    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]: ...


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\beaconing.py
# ==========================================
"""
BeaconingDetector вЂ” РїРµСЂРёРѕРґРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ РѕР±СЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ Рє C2 (heartbeat).

РџСЂРёР·РЅР°Рє: РјРЅРѕР¶РµСЃС‚РІРѕ TCP-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёР№ Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port)
СЃ СЂРµРіСѓР»СЏСЂРЅС‹Рј РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»РѕРј. C2-heartbeat вЂ” Р·Р°РїСЂРѕРіСЂР°РјРјРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ
СЃ РјР°Р»С‹Рј jitter.

РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ: Р·Р°СЂР°Р¶С‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ С…РѕСЃС‚ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ 60 СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ (В±2) СЃС‚СѓС‡РёС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° C2.
РЎРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёРµ (heartbeat), РЅРѕ СЂРµРіСѓР»СЏСЂРЅС‹Рµ.

Р›РѕРіРёРєР° (0.7.0):
- Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂРѕРІРєР° FlowKey РїРѕ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port).
- N >= beaconing_min_connections (20).
- Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ >= beaconing_min_duration_sec (600).
- jitter = stddev(intervals) / mean(intervals) < 0.1.
- РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ flow < beaconing_max_avg_bytes (5000).
- Rate = count / duration_min <= beaconing_max_flows_per_minute (5).
- stddev(payload)/mean(payload) <= beaconing_payload_stddev_ratio_max (0.5).
- dst_ip РЅРµ internal (РїРѕ С„Р»Р°РіСѓ beaconing_skip_internal_dst).
- dst_port РЅРµ РІ mail-РїРѕСЂС‚Р°С… (POP3/IMAP).
- Р“СЂСѓРїРїР° РЅРµ multi-tenant: <= 1 СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ SNI/Host.
- Р•РґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ SNI/Host РЅРµ РІ allowlist.

Р§С‚Рѕ РѕС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ:
- Mail polling (POP3/IMAP) вЂ” РїРѕСЂС‚-С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ.
- Chrome keepalive Рє CDN/analytics вЂ” multi-tenant filter +
  SNI/Host allowlist.
- Windows Update, Apple CDN вЂ” SNI/Host allowlist.
- Burst-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ (Chrome parallel fetch) вЂ” rate check.
- Browser keepalive СЃ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј payload вЂ” payload variance.

Р§С‚Рѕ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ:
- C2 РЅР° РѕРґРЅРѕРј РґРѕРјРµРЅРµ/IP: РѕРґРёРЅ SNI (РёР»Рё РЅРµС‚ SNI), const payload,
  РЅРёР·РєРёР№ jitter, РґРѕР»РіРёР№ РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚.

Confidence: 0.85 РїСЂРё jitter в†’ 0, 0.6 РїСЂРё jitter в†’ max_jitter_ratio.

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ brute_force:
  - brute_force вЂ” РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С†РµР»Рё Р—Рђ РљРћР РћРўРљРћР• Р’Р Р•РњРЇ.
  - beaconing   вЂ” РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С†РµР»Рё СЃ Р РђР’РќР«РњР РРќРўР•Р Р’РђР›РђРњР
                  РЅР° РґР»РёРЅРЅРѕРј РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Рµ.

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ port_scan:
  - port_scan вЂ” РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… dst РЅР° РѕРґРёРЅ РїРѕСЂС‚.
  - beaconing вЂ” РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows Рє РћР”РќРћР™ РїР°СЂРµ (dst_ip, dst_port).
"""
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding
from paradox.net_utils import is_internal_ip


@dataclass
class _BeaconGroup:
    first_timestamps: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)
    per_flow_bytes: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    sni_set: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    host_set: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    total_bytes: int = 0
    total_packets: int = 0
    count: int = 0

    @property
    def duration(self) -> float:
        if len(self.first_timestamps) < 2:
            return 0.0
        return max(self.first_timestamps) - min(self.first_timestamps)

    def avg_bytes(self) -> float:
        if self.count == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self.total_bytes / self.count

    def payload_stddev_ratio(self) -> float | None:
        """
        stddev/mean РїРѕ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂСѓ payload.

        C2: const payload в†’ 0.0.
        Browser: СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рµ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹ в†’ > 0.5.

        Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ None, РµСЃР»Рё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… < 5 flow.
        """
        if len(self.per_flow_bytes) < 5:
            return None
        mean = statistics.mean(self.per_flow_bytes)
        if mean <= 0:
            return None
        return statistics.pstdev(self.per_flow_bytes) / mean

    def unique_destinations(self) -> set[str]:
        """
        РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ SNI + HTTP Host РІ РіСЂСѓРїРїРµ.

        0 вЂ” РЅРµС‚ РЅРё SNI, РЅРё Host (raw IP, РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ C2).
        1 вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ РґРѕРјРµРЅ (C2 РР›Р РѕРґРЅРѕРґРѕРјРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃ).
        >1 вЂ” shared CDN/edge (Chrome Рє Akamai, multi-tenant edge).
        """
        return self.sni_set | self.host_set


def _suffix_match(value: str, suffixes: frozenset[str]) -> bool:
    """
    endswith-РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РїРѕ РґРѕРјРµРЅРЅРѕРјСѓ СЃСѓС„С„РёРєСЃСѓ.

    'www.google-analytics.com' matches 'google-analytics.com'.
    'notgoogle-analytics.com' does NOT match (РЅСѓР¶РЅР° С‚РѕС‡РєР° РїРµСЂРµРґ).
    """
    if not value:
        return False
    v = value.lower().rstrip(".")
    return any(v == s or v.endswith("." + s) for s in suffixes)


class BeaconingDetector:
    name = "beaconing"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_conn = config.beaconing_min_connections
        min_duration = config.beaconing_min_duration_sec
        max_jitter = config.beaconing_max_jitter_ratio
        max_avg_bytes = config.beaconing_max_avg_bytes
        max_rate = config.beaconing_max_flows_per_minute
        max_payload_var = config.beaconing_payload_stddev_ratio_max
        skip_internal = config.beaconing_skip_internal_dst
        mail_ports = config.beaconing_mail_polling_ports
        allow_suffixes = config.beaconing_allowlist_suffixes
        allow_ips = config.beaconing_allowlist_ips

        groups: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _BeaconGroup] = defaultdict(
            _BeaconGroup
        )

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if flow.first_ts is None:
                continue

            # Mail-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹: POP3/IMAP РїРѕР»Р»РёРЅРі = design, РЅРµ C2.
            # SMTP (25/465/587) РќР• Р·РґРµСЃСЊ вЂ” Р±С‹РІР°РµС‚ C2-РєР°РЅР°Р».
            if key.dst_port in mail_ports:
                continue

            # РўРѕС‡РµС‡РЅС‹Р№ IP allowlist (analytics Р±РµР· SNI).
            if key.dst_ip in allow_ips:
                continue

            # internal в†’ internal вЂ” РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ.
            if skip_internal and is_internal_ip(key.dst_ip, config):
                continue

            group = groups[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            group.first_timestamps.append(flow.first_ts)
            group.per_flow_bytes.append(flow.bytes_sent)
            group.total_bytes += flow.bytes_sent
            group.total_packets += flow.packets
            group.count += 1
            if flow.sni:
                group.sni_set.add(flow.sni.lower())
            if flow.http_host:
                group.host_set.add(flow.http_host.lower())

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), g in groups.items():
            if g.count < min_conn:
                continue
            if g.duration < min_duration:
                continue

            # Multi-tenant filter: > 1 СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ SNI/Host = shared edge.
            # Chrome Рє Akamai РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ РґРµСЃСЏС‚РєРё СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёР№ Рє РѕРґРЅРѕРјСѓ IP,
            # РЅРѕ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ вЂ” СЃ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј SNI. C2 РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ РћР”РРќ РґРѕРјРµРЅ.
            unique_dests = g.unique_destinations()
            if len(unique_dests) > 1:
                continue

            # SNI/Host allowlist: РѕРґРёРЅ dest, РЅРѕ РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ CDN/analytics.
            if len(unique_dests) == 1 and allow_suffixes:
                (single_dest,) = unique_dests
                if _suffix_match(single_dest, allow_suffixes):
                    continue

            # Rate check: Chrome РїР°СЂР°Р»Р»РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ РїР°С‡РєСѓ.
            duration_min = g.duration / 60.0
            if duration_min <= 0:
                continue
            flow_rate = g.count / duration_min
            if flow_rate > max_rate:
                continue

            ts_sorted = sorted(g.first_timestamps)
            intervals = [
                ts_sorted[i + 1] - ts_sorted[i]
                for i in range(len(ts_sorted) - 1)
            ]
            intervals = [iv for iv in intervals if iv > 0]
            if len(intervals) < min_conn - 1:
                continue

            mean_iv = statistics.mean(intervals)
            if mean_iv <= 0:
                continue
            stdev_iv = statistics.pstdev(intervals)
            jitter = stdev_iv / mean_iv

            if jitter >= max_jitter:
                continue

            avg_bytes = g.avg_bytes()
            if avg_bytes > max_avg_bytes:
                continue

            payload_var = g.payload_stddev_ratio()
            if payload_var is not None and payload_var > max_payload_var:
                continue

            confidence = 0.6 + 0.25 * (1 - jitter / max_jitter)
            confidence = max(0.6, min(0.85, confidence))

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="beaconing",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=round(confidence, 3),
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "count": g.count,
                    "duration_sec": round(g.duration, 1),
                    "flow_rate_per_min": round(flow_rate, 2),
                    "mean_interval_sec": round(mean_iv, 2),
                    "stdev_interval_sec": round(stdev_iv, 3),
                    "jitter_ratio": round(jitter, 3),
                    "avg_bytes_per_conn": round(avg_bytes, 1),
                    "payload_stddev_ratio": (
                        round(payload_var, 3)
                        if payload_var is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                    "unique_destinations": sorted(unique_dests),
                    "total_bytes": g.total_bytes,
                    "total_packets": g.total_packets,
                },
                first_seen=ts_sorted[0],
                last_seen=ts_sorted[-1],
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
BruteForceDetector вЂ” РїРѕРёСЃРє РїРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂР° Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ dst_ip:dst_port.

РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»: РјРЅРѕРіРѕ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёС… flows СЃ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src_ip Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С†РµР»Рё.

РќРµ РїСѓС‚Р°С‚СЊ СЃ:
  - port scan       в†’ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ dst_port, РїРѕ 1-3 РїР°РєРµС‚Р°
  - РґР»РёРЅРЅРѕР№ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРµР№ в†’ 1 flow, РјРЅРѕРіРѕ РїР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ
  - РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рј HTTP    в†’ flows СЂР°СЃРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅС‹ РїРѕ РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё, СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… dst
  - С‚СЂР°С„РёРєРѕРј Рє DC   в†’ LDAP/Kerberos/SMB вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРѕСЂРјР°
  - Google Safe Browsing в†’ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows СЃ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹РјРё URI
  - Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂРѕРј Рє CDN в†’ РјР°СЃСЃРѕРІС‹Рµ TCP-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РЅР° 443

Р›РѕРіРёРєР°:
  1. Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂСѓРµРј flows РїРѕ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port).
  2. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј DC-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ (LDAP/Kerberos/SMB).
  3. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј TLS-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ (443/8443) вЂ” Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ Рє CDN, РЅРµ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ.
  4. РЎС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј: flow_count, unique_src_ports, avg_packets,
     СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ URI РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё (strip query string).
  5. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј РїРѕ ratio unique_uris / flow_count (РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С…
     URI = РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°, РЅРµ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ).
  6. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј РїРѕ rate: СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј СЂРµРґРєРѕ в†’ РїРѕР»Р»РёРЅРі.
  7. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј РїРѕ login-hint: HTTP-С‚СЂР°С„РёРє Р±РµР· login endpoint
     РІ URI вЂ” Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ РєР°С‡Р°РµС‚ РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєРё, РЅРµ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ.
  8. РџРѕСЂРѕРіРё: count >= min_flows, avg_packets <= max_avg,
     unique_src_ports >= min_unique_src_ports.

РљР°Р¶РґР°СЏ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° Р°СѓС‚РµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё = РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ src_port.
РџРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ unique_src_ports в‰€ count flows вЂ” РІС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ РЅРµР·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјС‹Р№
РїСЂРёР·РЅР°Рє РїРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂР°, Р° РЅРµ РґР»РёРЅРЅРѕР№ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё.

РР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅРѕРµ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅРёРµ: SYN-СЃРєР°РЅ СЃ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src РЅР° РѕРґРёРЅ dst:port
С‚РѕР¶Рµ РїРѕС…РѕР¶ РЅР° СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅ. РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёС‚СЊ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕ РЅР°Р»РёС‡РёСЋ
SYN+ACK вЂ” СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° РІ FlowStats РЅРµС‚. РЎРј. TODO.
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    """РђРіСЂРµРіР°С‚ flows РґР»СЏ РїР°СЂС‹ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port)."""
    flow_count: int = 0
    packets_total: int = 0
    bytes_sent_total: int = 0
    src_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    uris: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


def _normalize_uri(uri: str | None) -> str | None:
    """РЎСЂРµР·Р°С‚СЊ query string РґР»СЏ СЃСЂР°РІРЅРµРЅРёСЏ endpoint'РѕРІ."""
    if not uri:
        return None
    if "?" in uri:
        uri = uri.split("?", 1)[0]
    return uri or None


class BruteForceDetector:
    name = "brute_force"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_ports = config.brute_force_excluded_ports
        skip_tls_ports = config.brute_force_skip_tls_ports
        login_hints = config.login_endpoint_hints

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            # DC-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹: РјРЅРѕРіРѕ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёС… Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ Рє DC вЂ” РЅРѕСЂРјР°.
            if key.dst_port in excluded_ports:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1
            bucket.packets_total += flow.packets
            bucket.bytes_sent_total += flow.bytes_sent
            if key.src_port is not None:
                bucket.src_ports.add(key.src_port)

            uri = _normalize_uri(flow.http_uri)
            if uri:
                bucket.uris.add(uri)

            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), bucket in agg.items():
            flow_count = bucket.flow_count
            if flow_count < config.brute_force_min_flows:
                continue

            avg_packets = bucket.packets_total / flow_count
            if avg_packets > config.brute_force_max_avg_packets:
                continue

            unique_src_ports = len(bucket.src_ports)
            if unique_src_ports < config.brute_force_min_unique_src_ports:
                continue

            # --- Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ 1: ratio СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… URI ---
            # РњРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… URI = РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р° (Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ).
            unique_uris = bucket.uris
            if (unique_uris
                    and len(unique_uris) / flow_count
                    > config.brute_force_uri_ratio_threshold):
                continue

            duration = 0.0
            if bucket.first_seen is not None and bucket.last_seen is not None:
                duration = bucket.last_seen - bucket.first_seen

            # --- Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ 2: rate ---
            # РЎР»РёС€РєРѕРј СЂРµРґРєРѕ = РїРѕР»Р»РёРЅРі, РЅРµ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ.
            if duration > 0:
                rate = flow_count / duration
                if rate < config.brute_force_min_rate_flows_per_sec:
                    continue

            # --- Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ 3: TLS-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ ---
            # 443/8443 вЂ” РјР°СЃСЃРѕРІС‹Рµ TCP-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ = Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ Рє CDN.
            if dst_port in skip_tls_ports:
                continue

            # --- Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ 4: login endpoint РІ URI ---
            # Р•СЃР»Рё HTTP-С‚СЂР°С„РёРє Рё РЅРё РѕРґРёРЅ URI РЅРµ РїРѕС…РѕР¶ РЅР° login
            # endpoint вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЏ. Р•СЃР»Рё URI РЅРµС‚ СЃРѕРІСЃРµРј
            # (SSH/FTP/RDP) вЂ” С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РЅРµ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ.
            if unique_uris:
                has_login = any(
                    hint in uri.lower()
                    for uri in unique_uris
                    for hint in login_hints
                )
                if not has_login:
                    continue

            # --- Confidence ---
            flow_score = min(1.0, flow_count / 100.0)
            port_score = min(1.0, unique_src_ports / 50.0)
            confidence = round(0.5 + 0.3 * flow_score + 0.15 * port_score, 3)
            confidence = min(confidence, 0.95)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="brute_force",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "flow_count": flow_count,
                    "unique_src_ports": unique_src_ports,
                    "unique_uris": len(unique_uris),
                    "packets_total": bucket.packets_total,
                    "bytes_sent_total": bucket.bytes_sent_total,
                    "avg_packets_per_flow": round(avg_packets, 2),
                    "duration_sec": round(duration, 2),
                    "rate_flows_per_sec": (
                        round(flow_count / duration, 6)
                        if duration > 0 else None
                    ),
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\cloud_tunnel.py
# ==========================================
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class CloudTunnelDetector:
    """
    Р”РѕРјРµРЅС‹ РЅР° СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР°С…, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ СЃРєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ C2:
    trycloudflare, ngrok, serveo, localtunnel Рё С‚.Рї.
    """
    name = "cloud_tunnel"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for name, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            match = self._match_tunnel(name, config)
            if match is None:
                continue

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="cloud_tunnel_domain",
                value=name,
                confidence=0.7,
                evidence={
                    "matched_suffix": match,
                    "queries": count,
                },
            ))

        return findings

    def _match_tunnel(self, name: str, config: Config) -> str | None:
        for suffix in config.cloud_tunnel_domains:
            if name == suffix or name.endswith("." + suffix):
                return suffix
        return None


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\command_injection.py
# ==========================================
"""
CommandInjectionDetector вЂ” RCE С‡РµСЂРµР· HTTP URI РёР»Рё POST body.

Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕРІРµСЂС… state.http_objects (Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ HTTPObjectExtractor
РёР· СЃРєР»РµРµРЅРЅС‹С… TCP-РїРѕС‚РѕРєРѕРІ). РС‰РµС‚ РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅС‹ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґ Рё СЏР·С‹РєРѕРІС‹Рµ
РєРѕРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРєС†РёРё РІ:
  - URI Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° (query string, path)
  - body Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° (form-urlencoded РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ)

РџСЂРёР·РЅР°РєРё RCE:
  - io.popen / os.execute (Lua)
  - system( / exec( / shell_exec( (PHP)
  - os.system / subprocess (Python)
  - $(...) / backticks (shell)
  - cmd.exe / /bin/sh + wget/curl (РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёРєР°)

РђРіСЂРµРіР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕ (src_ip, uri). РћРґРёРЅ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ СЃ RCE = РѕРґРёРЅ finding.
Р•СЃР»Рё РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ N С‚РµР»Р°С… вЂ” count = N, РЅРѕ finding РѕРґРёРЅ.

Р›РѕР¶РЅС‹Рµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РЅРёСЏ:
  - HTML/JS РІ body СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ eval() вЂ” С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ kind="request"
  - JSON СЃ "system" РІ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРё вЂ” РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїСЂРѕР№С‚Рё, РµСЃР»Рё РЅРµС‚ "(".
    РџРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ "system(" (СЃРѕ СЃРєРѕР±РєРѕР№) вЂ” high confidence.

Confidence:
  - high-РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅ в†’ 0.9
  - С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ medium-РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅС‹ (в‰Ґ2) в†’ 0.75
  - С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ medium в†’ 0.6
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from urllib.parse import unquote


from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    count: int = 0
    matched_high: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter)
    matched_medium: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter)
    body_samples: list[bytes] = field(default_factory=list)
    methods: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    hosts: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    dst_ips: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    dst_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None


class CommandInjectionDetector:
    name = "command_injection"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        high = config.command_injection_patterns_high
        medium = config.command_injection_patterns_medium
        min_patterns = config.command_injection_min_patterns

        buckets: dict[tuple[str, str], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, obj in (state.http_objects or []):
            if obj.kind != "request":
                continue

            # РўРµРєСЃС‚ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРёСЃРєР°: URI + body (РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№)
            haystack_parts: list[str] = []
            if obj.uri:
                haystack_parts.append(obj.uri)
                # РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚ вЂ” %3d в†’ "=", %2f в†’ "/"
                try:
                    haystack_parts.append(unquote(obj.uri))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if obj.body:
                # iso-8859-1 вЂ” РІСЃРµ 256 Р±Р°Р№С‚ в†’ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»С‹, Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРёСЃРєР°
                raw_text = obj.body.decode("iso-8859-1", errors="ignore")
                haystack_parts.append(raw_text)
                # url-decode (form-urlencoded)
                try:
                    haystack_parts.append(unquote(raw_text))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if not haystack_parts:
                continue

            haystack = "\n".join(haystack_parts)
            haystack_lc = haystack.lower()

            found_high: list[str] = [
                p for p in high if p.lower() in haystack_lc
            ]
            found_medium: list[str] = [
                p for p in medium if p.lower() in haystack_lc
            ]

            total = len(found_high) + len(found_medium)
            if total < min_patterns:
                continue
            # Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ high, С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµРј С…РѕС‚СЏ Р±С‹ 2 medium вЂ”
            # РѕРґРЅР° РїРѕРґРѕР·СЂРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј С€СѓРјРЅРѕ.
            if not found_high and len(found_medium) < 2:
                continue

            # РљР»СЋС‡ Р°РіСЂРµРіР°С†РёРё: (src_ip, uri)
            agg_key = (key.src_ip, obj.uri or "")
            b = buckets[agg_key]
            b.count += 1
            for p in found_high:
                b.matched_high[p] += 1
            for p in found_medium:
                b.matched_medium[p] += 1
            if len(b.body_samples) < 3 and obj.body:
                b.body_samples.append(obj.body[:256])
            if obj.method:
                b.methods.add(obj.method)
            if obj.headers and obj.headers.get("host"):
                b.hosts.add(obj.headers["host"])
            if key.dst_ip:
                b.dst_ips.add(key.dst_ip)
            if key.dst_port is not None:
                b.dst_ports.add(key.dst_port)

            # Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРј timestamps РёР· flow.
            # РћРґРёРЅ URI РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїСЂРёР№С‚Рё С‡РµСЂРµР· РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ flows вЂ”
            # Р±РµСЂС‘Рј min(first_ts) Рё max(last_ts).
            flow = state.flows.get(key)
            if flow is not None:
                if flow.first_ts is not None:
                    if b.first_seen is None or flow.first_ts < b.first_seen:
                        b.first_seen = flow.first_ts
                if flow.last_ts is not None:
                    if b.last_seen is None or flow.last_ts > b.last_seen:
                        b.last_seen = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, uri), b in buckets.items():
            if b.matched_high:
                confidence = 0.9
            elif sum(b.matched_medium.values()) >= 2:
                confidence = 0.75
            else:
                confidence = 0.6

            all_patterns: list[str] = sorted(
                set(b.matched_high) | set(b.matched_medium)
            )
            dst_ips_sorted = sorted(b.dst_ips)
            evidence = {
                "src_ip": src_ip,
                "uri": uri,
                "count": b.count,
                "matched_high": sorted(b.matched_high.keys()),
                "matched_medium": sorted(b.matched_medium.keys()),
                "matched_patterns": all_patterns,
                "methods": sorted(b.methods),
                "hosts": sorted(b.hosts)[:5],
                "dst_ip": dst_ips_sorted[0] if dst_ips_sorted else None,
                "dst_ips": dst_ips_sorted,
                "dst_ports": sorted(b.dst_ports),
            }
            if b.body_samples:
                evidence["body_samples"] = [
                    s.decode("iso-8859-1", errors="replace")[:200]
                    for s in b.body_samples
                ]

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="command_injection",
                value=f"{src_ip} в†’ {uri}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence=evidence,
                first_seen=b.first_seen,
                last_seen=b.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\dga.py
# ==========================================
import math
from collections import Counter

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


def shannon_entropy(s: str) -> float:
    if not s:
        return 0.0
    counts = Counter(s)
    n = len(s)
    return -sum((c / n) * math.log2(c / n) for c in counts.values())


class DGADetector:
    name = "dga"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []

        for name, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            if self._is_allowlisted(name, config):
                continue

            label = name.split(".")[0]

            if len(label) < config.dga_min_label_length:
                continue

            # DGA-РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ РїРѕС‡С‚Рё РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶Р°С‚ РґРµС„РёСЃРѕРІ.
            # Р”РµС„РёСЃ вЂ” РїСЂРёР·РЅР°Рє С‡РµР»РѕРІРµРєРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РµРјРѕРіРѕ РґРѕРјРµРЅР°
            # (safebrowsing-cache.google.com Рё С‚.Рї.).
            if "-" in label:
                continue

            entropy = shannon_entropy(label)
            if entropy < config.dga_entropy_threshold:
                continue

            confidence = min(
                0.95,
                0.5 + (entropy - config.dga_entropy_threshold) / 2.0,
            )

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="dga_domain",
                value=name,
                confidence=round(confidence, 3),
                evidence={
                    "label": label,
                    "entropy": round(entropy, 3),
                    "queries": count,
                },
            ))

        return findings

    def _is_allowlisted(self, name: str, config: Config) -> bool:
        return any(name.endswith(suffix) for suffix in config.dga_allowlist)


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\dns_tunneling.py
# ==========================================
"""
DNSTunnelingDetector вЂ” DNS-С‚СѓРЅРЅРµР»РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ (exfiltration С‡РµСЂРµР· DNS).

РџСЂРёР·РЅР°Рє: РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹Рµ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ РїРѕРґ РѕРґРЅРёРј Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рј РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРј
РѕС‚ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ РєР»РёРµРЅС‚Р°. Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРґРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ qname (base64, hex, custom).

РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ:
  aGVsbG8gd29ybGQ.dns.attacker.com
  d29ybGQgaGVsbG8.dns.attacker.com
  ...

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ DGA:
  - DGA вЂ” РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёРµ РїРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ (10-15 СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ), РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С…
  - Tunneling вЂ” РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ (>50), С‚РѕР¶Рµ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ
РўСЂРµР±СѓРµРј min_qname_length, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ DGA РЅРµ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р» РєР°Рє tunneling.

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ cloud_tunnel_domain:
  - cloud_tunnel_domain вЂ” РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃС‹ (trycloudflare, ngrok)
  - DNS tunneling вЂ” СЃР°РјРѕРґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕС‚РѕРєРѕР» С‡РµСЂРµР· DNS

Confidence: 0.9 РїСЂРё РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹С… qname + РјРЅРѕРіРѕ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ, 0.7 РёРЅР°С‡Рµ.
"""
from collections import defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


def _extract_base_domain(qname: str) -> str:
    """v0.9.0: СѓС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ РґРІСѓС…СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРІС‹Рµ TLD (co.uk, com.au)."""
    from paradox.domain_utils import base_domain
    return base_domain(qname)


class DNSTunnelingDetector:
    name = "dns_tunneling"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_len = config.dns_tunneling_min_qname_length
        min_long = config.dns_tunneling_min_long_qnames
        min_queries = config.dns_tunneling_min_queries
        allowlist = config.dns_tunneling_allowlist

        findings: list[Finding] = []

        # Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ (src_ip, base_domain)
        # value: set[qname] Рё total_queries
        groups: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"qnames": set(), "total_queries": 0, "long_qnames": set()}
        )

        for src_ip, queries in state.dns_queries_by_src.items():
            for qname, count in queries.items():
                qname_lc = qname.lower().strip(".")
                if not qname_lc:
                    continue
                base = _extract_base_domain(qname_lc)

                # Allowlist (DoH Рё РїСЂ.) вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј
                if any(
                    qname_lc == a or qname_lc.endswith("." + a)
                    for a in allowlist
                ):
                    continue

                key = (src_ip, base)
                groups[key]["qnames"].add(qname_lc)
                groups[key]["total_queries"] += count
                if len(qname_lc) >= min_len:
                    groups[key]["long_qnames"].add(qname_lc)

        for (src_ip, base), data in groups.items():
            if data["total_queries"] < min_queries:
                continue
            if len(data["long_qnames"]) < min_long:
                continue

            long_count = len(data["long_qnames"])
            total_unique = len(data["qnames"])

            confidence = 0.9 if long_count >= min_long * 2 else 0.7

            # РЎСЌРјРїР» РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹С… qname (РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 5 РїРѕ Р°Р»С„Р°РІРёС‚Сѓ)
            samples = sorted(data["long_qnames"])[:5]

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="dns_tunneling",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {base}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "base_domain": base,
                    "total_queries": data["total_queries"],
                    "unique_qnames": total_unique,
                    "long_qnames_count": long_count,
                    "min_qname_length": min_len,
                    "samples": samples,
                },
                first_seen=None,
                last_seen=None,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\explainer.py
# ==========================================
"""РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РѕР±СЉСЏСЃРЅРµРЅРёРµ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё (confidence) РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ.

Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ build_reasons РїСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ evidence Рё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃС‚СЂРѕРє,
РѕРїРёСЃС‹РІР°СЋС‰РёС…, РЅР° РѕСЃРЅРѕРІР°РЅРёРё С‡РµРіРѕ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ СЃРґРµР»Р°Р» РІС‹РІРѕРґ.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

# РЎР»РѕРІР°СЂСЊ: РєР»СЋС‡ РІ evidence -> С‡РµР»РѕРІРµРєРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РµРјРѕРµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ
LABELS = {
    "packets_total": "Р’СЃРµРіРѕ РїР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ",
    "unique_src_ports": "РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… src-РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ",
    "unique_dst_ports": "РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… dst-РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ",
    "flow_count": "РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ РїРѕС‚РѕРєРѕРІ",
    "bytes_total": "Р’СЃРµРіРѕ Р±Р°Р№С‚",
    "attempts": "РџРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє",
    "queries": "Р—Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ",
    "nxdomain_count": "NXDOMAIN-РѕС‚РІРµС‚РѕРІ",
    "duration": "Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ (СЃРµРє)",
    "packets": "РџР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ",
    "bytes_sent": "Р‘Р°Р№С‚ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРѕ",
    "bytes_recv": "Р‘Р°Р№С‚ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРѕ",
    "dst_ports": "Р¦РµР»РµРІС‹С… РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ",
    "src_ips": "РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєРѕРІ",
    "dst_ips": "РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёР№",
    "domains": "Р”РѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ",
    "entropy": "Р­РЅС‚СЂРѕРїРёСЏ",
    "payload_size": "Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ payload",
    "sample": "РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ",
    "rule": "YARA-РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рѕ",
    "tags": "РўРµРіРё",
    "filename": "РРјСЏ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°",
    "ja3": "JA3-РѕС‚РїРµС‡Р°С‚РѕРє",
    "ja3s": "JA3S-РѕС‚РїРµС‡Р°С‚РѕРє",
    "sni": "SNI",
    "user_agent": "User-Agent",
    "host": "РҐРѕСЃС‚",
    "uri": "URI",
    "method": "HTTP-РјРµС‚РѕРґ",
}

def build_reasons(evidence: dict) -> list[str]:
    if not evidence:
        return []

    reasons = []
    # 1. РР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ РєР»СЋС‡Рё
    for k, v in evidence.items():
        if k in LABELS:
            reasons.append(f"{LABELS[k]}: {v}")

    # 2. Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°С€Р»Рё (РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ РєР»СЋС‡Рё) вЂ” РІС‹РІРѕРґРёРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 3 РїР°СЂС‹
    if not reasons:
        for k, v in list(evidence.items())[:3]:
            reasons.append(f"{k}: {v}")

    return reasons



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\ftp_brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
FTPBruteForceDetector вЂ” РїРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂ РїР°СЂРѕР»РµР№ Рє FTP-СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂСѓ.

РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»: РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port=21) СЃ
СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹РјРё src_ports. РљР°Р¶РґР°СЏ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° Р»РѕРіРёРЅР° = РЅРѕРІРѕРµ TCP-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёРµ
(РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ src_port), РїРѕС‚РѕРј Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‚РєР°Р·Р°. 100+ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ вЂ”
РѕРґРЅРѕР·РЅР°С‡РЅС‹Р№ СЃР»РѕРІР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂ.

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ HTTP-brute_force:
  - HTTP-РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ СЂСѓР±РёС‚ avg_packets > 20 (FTP-СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЏ = 1185 РїР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ)
  - HTTP-РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ login-hint РІ URI, Сѓ FTP URI РЅРµС‚

Confidence:
  - 0.95 РїСЂРё flow_count >= 100
  - 0.85 РїСЂРё flow_count >= 50
  - 0.75 РёРЅР°С‡Рµ (РЅРѕ >= ftp_brute_force_min_flows)
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    flow_count: int = 0
    src_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    packets_total: int = 0
    bytes_total: int = 0
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


class FTPBruteForceDetector:
    name = "ftp_brute_force"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        min_flows = config.ftp_brute_force_min_flows
        min_ports = config.ftp_brute_force_min_unique_src_ports

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            if key.dst_port != 21:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1
            if key.src_port is not None:
                bucket.src_ports.add(key.src_port)
            bucket.packets_total += flow.packets
            bucket.bytes_total += flow.bytes_sent
            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip), bucket in agg.items():
            if bucket.flow_count < min_flows:
                continue
            if len(bucket.src_ports) < min_ports:
                continue

            if bucket.flow_count >= 100:
                confidence = 0.95
            elif bucket.flow_count >= 50:
                confidence = 0.85
            else:
                confidence = 0.75

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ftp_brute_force",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:21",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": 21,
                    "flow_count": bucket.flow_count,
                    "unique_src_ports": len(bucket.src_ports),
                    "packets_total": bucket.packets_total,
                    "bytes_total": bucket.bytes_total,
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\ip_check_service.py
# ==========================================
"""
IPCheckServiceDetector вЂ” РѕР±СЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ Рє СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР°Рј РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё РІРЅРµС€РЅРµРіРѕ IP
(ip-api.com, ifconfig.me, ipinfo.io). РўРёРїРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ С€Р°Рі malware Рё СЃРєСЂРёРїС‚РѕРІ
РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРЅРµС‚-РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїР° РїРµСЂРµРґ C2.

РњР°С‚С‡РёРЅРі РїРѕ С‡РµС‚С‹СЂС‘Рј РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР°Рј:
  - DNS-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ Рє РґРѕРјРµРЅСѓ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР° (state.dns_queries)
  - SNI РІ TLS ClientHello (flow.sni)
  - HTTP Host РІ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРµ (flow.http_host)
  - РџСЂСЏРјРѕР№ IP-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ (flow.dst_ip РІ config.ip_check_service_ips)

Р’СЃРµ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєРё РґР°СЋС‚ РѕРґРЅРѕ Рё С‚Рѕ Р¶Рµ value вЂ” РєР°РЅРѕРЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РёРјСЏ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР°
(РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, "ip-api.com"). Р•СЃР»Рё РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ СЂРµР·РѕР»РІРёС‚ РґРѕРјРµРЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· DNS Рё РїРѕС‚РѕРј
СЃС‚СѓС‡РёС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ IP вЂ” РІСЃРµ С‚СЂРё РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР° СЃРѕР»СЊСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ РѕРґРёРЅ finding
СЃ sources={dns, sni, direct_ip}. Р­С‚Рѕ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅС‹Рј.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class IPCheckServiceDetector:
    name = "ip_check_service"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        services = config.ip_check_services
        service_ips = config.ip_check_service_ips

        # value (РёРјСЏ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР°) -> {sources: set, extra: dict}
        hits: dict[str, dict] = {}

        def _touch(value: str, source: str, extra: dict | None = None) -> None:
            h = hits.setdefault(value, {"sources": set(), "extra": {}})
            h["sources"].add(source)
            if extra:
                h["extra"].update(extra)

        # 1. DNS
        for qname, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            domain = qname.lower().strip(".")
            if domain in services:
                _touch(domain, "dns", {"queries": count})

        # 2. SNI / 3. HTTP Host / 4. direct IP
        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if flow.sni:
                sni = flow.sni.lower().strip(".")
                if sni in services:
                    _touch(sni, "sni",
                           {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

            if flow.http_host:
                host = flow.http_host.split(":", 1)[0].lower().strip(".")
                if host in services:
                    _touch(host, "http_host",
                           {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

            # direct IP вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј РєР°РЅРѕРЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РёРјСЏ СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР° РєР°Рє value,
            # С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ finding СЃР»РёР»СЃСЏ СЃ DNS/SNI-РЅР°С…РѕРґРєР°РјРё
            service_name = service_ips.get(key.dst_ip)
            if service_name:
                _touch(service_name, "direct_ip",
                       {"flow": f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}"})

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for value, h in hits.items():
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ip_check_service",
                value=value,
                confidence=0.7,
                evidence={
                    "sources": sorted(h["sources"]),
                    **h["extra"],
                },
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: f.value)
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\ja3_malware.py
# ==========================================
"""
JA3MalwareDetector вЂ” РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ JA3-РѕС‚РїРµС‡Р°С‚РєРё malware/pentest.

РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµС‚ flow.ja3 (ClientHello fingerprint) РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІ СЃР»РѕРІР°СЂСЏ
РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹С… С…РµС€РµР№ РІ config.ja3_known_malware. Р•СЃР»Рё СЃРѕРІРїР°Р» вЂ” finding
ja3_malware СЃ РёРјРµРЅРµРј РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°.

РђРіСЂРµРіР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕ JA3: РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ flow'РѕРІ СЃ РѕРґРЅРёРј РѕС‚РїРµС‡Р°С‚РєРѕРј вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ finding
СЃ РїРµСЂРµС‡РЅРµРј СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… src/dst.

Confidence 0.85 вЂ” РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РїРµС‡Р°С‚РѕРє malware СЌС‚Рѕ СЃРёР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»,
РЅРѕ РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅС‹ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёСЏ СЃ Р»РµРіРёС‚РёРјРЅС‹РјРё РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏРјРё, РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‰РёРјРё
С‚Рµ Р¶Рµ Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё (python-requests, go-http-client).
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class JA3MalwareDetector:
    name = "ja3_malware"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        known = config.ja3_known_malware
        if not known:
            return []

        buckets: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(lambda: {
            "flow_count": 0,
            "src_ips": Counter(),
            "dst_ips": Counter(),
            "dst_ports": Counter(),
            "first_seen": None,
            "last_seen": None,
        })

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if not flow.ja3 or flow.ja3 not in known:
                continue
            b = buckets[flow.ja3]
            b["flow_count"] += 1
            b["src_ips"][key.src_ip] += 1
            b["dst_ips"][key.dst_ip] += 1
            if key.dst_port is not None:
                b["dst_ports"][key.dst_port] += 1
            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if b["first_seen"] is None or flow.first_ts < b["first_seen"]:
                    b["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if b["last_seen"] is None or flow.last_ts > b["last_seen"]:
                    b["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for ja3, b in buckets.items():
            name = known[ja3]
            top_srcs = [
                {"ip": ip, "count": c}
                for ip, c in b["src_ips"].most_common(5)
            ]
            top_dsts = [
                {"ip": ip, "count": c}
                for ip, c in b["dst_ips"].most_common(5)
            ]
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ja3_malware",
                value=f"{name} (ja3={ja3[:8]}вЂ¦)",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "ja3": ja3,
                    "tool_name": name,
                    "flow_count": b["flow_count"],
                    "top_src_ips": top_srcs,
                    "top_dst_ips": top_dsts,
                    "dst_ports": sorted(b["dst_ports"].keys()),
                },
                first_seen=b["first_seen"],
                last_seen=b["last_seen"],
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\large_transfer.py
# ==========================================
"""
LargeTransferDetector вЂ” Р°РЅРѕРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№ РѕР±СЉС‘Рј РІ РѕРґРЅРѕРј flow (РїР°СЂРµ).

РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»: РѕРґРёРЅ FlowKey (РёР»Рё РїР°СЂР° РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёР№) РїРµСЂРµРґР°Р» > large_transfer_min_bytes.

Р§С‚Рѕ РІР°Р¶РЅРѕ РґР»СЏ С‚СЂРёР°Р¶Р°:
  - РЅРµ В«РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ Р±Р°Р№С‚В», Р° РљРЈР”Рђ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ: outbound СЃ РїРµСЂРµРІРµСЃРѕРј РІРІРµСЂС… вЂ”
    СЌРєСЃС„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ (T1041), inbound вЂ” РІС‹РєР°С‡РєР° payload'Р°/РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ (T1105).
  - internal в†” internal (Р±СЌРєР°Рї, РјРёРіСЂР°С†РёСЏ) вЂ” РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ, РЅРµ C2.

РђРіСЂРµРіР°С†РёСЏ: РїР°СЂР° (ip, port) + (ip, port), РѕР±Р° РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РІ РѕРґРёРЅ bucket.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding, FlowKey
from paradox.net_utils import is_internal_ip


# Р•СЃР»Рё РѕРґРЅР° СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅР° >NГ— РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ вЂ” СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РѕР±РјРµРЅ РѕРґРЅРѕСЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅРЅРёРј.
# РџРѕСЂРѕРі В«РЅР° РіР»Р°Р·В»: symmetric TLS handshake + data РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ РґР°СЋС‚ в‰Ґ1:10.
_ASYMMETRY_RATIO = 5.0


def _pair_key(key: FlowKey) -> tuple:
    """
    РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РєР»СЋС‡ РїР°СЂС‹: (ip_a, port_a, ip_b, port_b, proto).
    РџСЂСЏРјРѕР№ Рё РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅС‹Р№ flow РґР°СЋС‚ РѕРґРёРЅР°РєРѕРІС‹Р№ РєР»СЋС‡.
    """
    a = (key.src_ip, key.src_port or 0)
    b = (key.dst_ip, key.dst_port or 0)
    if a <= b:
        return (a, b, key.proto)
    return (b, a, key.proto)


def _direction_for(src_ip: str, dst_ip: str, config: Config) -> str:
    """
    inbound  вЂ” РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№ в†’ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№
    outbound вЂ” РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ в†’ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№
    internal вЂ” РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ в†” РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№
    external вЂ” РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№ в†” РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№ (СЂРµРґРєРѕСЃС‚СЊ РІ enterprise pcap)
    """
    src_in = is_internal_ip(src_ip, config)
    dst_in = is_internal_ip(dst_ip, config)
    if src_in and dst_in:
        return "internal"
    if src_in:
        return "outbound"
    if dst_in:
        return "inbound"
    return "external"


def _mitre_hint_for(
    direction: str, bytes_in: int, bytes_out: int,
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """
    MITRE-РїРѕРґСЃРєР°Р·РєР° РїРѕ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЋ + Р°СЃРёРјРјРµС‚СЂРёРё.

    - outbound СЃ Р±РѕР»СЊС€РёРј upload в†’ T1041 (Exfil Over C2 Channel)
    - inbound СЃ Р±РѕР»СЊС€РёРј download в†’ T1105 (Ingress Tool Transfer)
    - internal в†” internal в†’ T1071.001 (Application Layer Protocol)
    - СЃРёРјРјРµС‚СЂРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№ РѕР±РјРµРЅ вЂ” Р±РµР· С‚РµС…РЅРёРєРё (T1071.001 СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РѕР±С‰РёР№).
    """
    if direction == "outbound" and bytes_out > bytes_in * _ASYMMETRY_RATIO:
        return ("T1041",)
    if direction == "inbound" and bytes_in > bytes_out * _ASYMMETRY_RATIO:
        return ("T1105",)
    if direction == "internal":
        return ("T1071.001",)
    return ()


class LargeTransferDetector:
    name = "large_transfer"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        threshold = config.large_transfer_min_bytes
        include_reverse = config.large_transfer_include_reverse

        if include_reverse:
            findings = self._run_pair_mode(state, config, threshold)
        else:
            findings = self._run_single_mode(state, config, threshold)

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: -f.evidence["total_bytes"])
        return findings

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # single mode вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ flow
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def _run_single_mode(
            self, state: CaptureState, config: Config, threshold: int,
    ) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_hosts = config.large_transfer_excluded_hosts
        excluded_uas = config.large_transfer_excluded_user_agents

        def _is_excluded(flow) -> bool:
            host = (flow.http_host or "").lower()
            ua = (flow.http_user_agent or "").lower()
            if any(h in host for h in excluded_hosts):
                return True
            if any(u.lower() in ua for u in excluded_uas):
                return True
            return False

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if _is_excluded(flow):
                continue
            if flow.bytes_sent < threshold:
                continue

            direction = _direction_for(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, config)
            # bytes_in/out РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ internal-СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅС‹.
            # Р’ single mode РІРёРґРёРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕРґРЅСѓ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ РїРѕС‚РѕРєР°.
            if direction == "outbound":
                bytes_out, bytes_in = flow.bytes_sent, 0
            elif direction == "inbound":
                bytes_out, bytes_in = 0, flow.bytes_sent
            else:
                # internal / external: РїРѕРЅСЏС‚РёСЏ in/out СЂР°Р·РјС‹С‚С‹,
                # РєР»Р°РґС‘Рј РІСЃС‘ РІ out вЂ” РІР°Р¶РЅРѕ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ direction.
                bytes_out, bytes_in = flow.bytes_sent, 0

            mitre = _mitre_hint_for(direction, bytes_in, bytes_out)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="large_transfer",
                value=f"{key.src_ip} -> {key.dst_ip}:{key.dst_port}",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": key.src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": key.dst_ip,
                    "src_port": key.src_port,
                    "dst_port": key.dst_port,
                    "proto": key.proto,
                    "total_bytes": flow.bytes_sent,
                    "bytes_in": bytes_in,
                    "bytes_out": bytes_out,
                    "packets": flow.packets,
                    "direction": direction,
                    "sni": flow.sni,
                },
                first_seen=flow.first_ts,
                last_seen=flow.last_ts,
                mitre_techniques=mitre,
            ))
        return findings

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------
    # pair mode вЂ” СЃСѓРјРјР° РїРѕ РѕР±РѕРёРј РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏРј
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def _run_pair_mode(
            self, state: CaptureState, config: Config, threshold: int,
    ) -> list[Finding]:
        excluded_hosts = config.large_transfer_excluded_hosts
        excluded_uas = config.large_transfer_excluded_user_agents

        def _is_excluded(flow) -> bool:
            host = (flow.http_host or "").lower()
            ua = (flow.http_user_agent or "").lower()
            if any(h in host for h in excluded_hosts):
                return True
            if any(u.lower() in ua for u in excluded_uas):
                return True
            return False

        buckets: dict[tuple, dict] = {}

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if _is_excluded(flow):
                continue
            pair = _pair_key(key)
            b = buckets.get(pair)
            if b is None:
                # Р¤РёРєСЃРёСЂСѓРµРј A-СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ = src РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРІРёРґРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ flow.
                # РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РІ state.flows РЅРµРґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ РјРµР¶РґСѓ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РјРё,
                # РЅРѕ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ bucket РІСЃС‘ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРѕРІР°РЅРѕ.
                b = {
                    "ip_a": key.src_ip, "port_a": key.src_port,
                    "ip_b": key.dst_ip, "port_b": key.dst_port,
                    "proto": key.proto,
                    "bytes_a_to_b": 0,
                    "bytes_b_to_a": 0,
                    "total_packets": 0,
                    "flows": 0,
                    "sni": None,
                    "first_seen": None,
                    "last_seen": None,
                }
                buckets[pair] = b

            if key.src_ip == b["ip_a"] and key.dst_ip == b["ip_b"]:
                b["bytes_a_to_b"] += flow.bytes_sent
            else:
                b["bytes_b_to_a"] += flow.bytes_sent

            b["total_packets"] += flow.packets
            b["flows"] += 1
            if flow.sni and not b["sni"]:
                b["sni"] = flow.sni
            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if b["first_seen"] is None or flow.first_ts < b["first_seen"]:
                    b["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if b["last_seen"] is None or flow.last_ts > b["last_seen"]:
                    b["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for b in buckets.values():
            total = b["bytes_a_to_b"] + b["bytes_b_to_a"]
            if total < threshold:
                continue

            direction = _direction_for(b["ip_a"], b["ip_b"], config)

            # РџСЂРёРІРѕРґРёРј bytes_in/out Рє internal-РїРµСЂСЃРїРµРєС‚РёРІРµ.
            if direction == "inbound":
                # ip_a вЂ” РІРЅРµС€РЅРёР№, ip_b вЂ” internal: aв†’b СЌС‚Рѕ inbound
                bytes_in = b["bytes_a_to_b"]
                bytes_out = b["bytes_b_to_a"]
            else:
                # outbound / internal / external: СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј aв†’b Р·Р° out
                bytes_out = b["bytes_a_to_b"]
                bytes_in = b["bytes_b_to_a"]

            mitre = _mitre_hint_for(direction, bytes_in, bytes_out)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="large_transfer",
                value=f"{b['ip_a']} <-> {b['ip_b']}:{b['port_b']}",
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": b["ip_a"],
                    "dst_ip": b["ip_b"],
                    "src_port": b["port_a"],
                    "dst_port": b["port_b"],
                    "proto": b["proto"],
                    "total_bytes": total,
                    "bytes_in": bytes_in,
                    "bytes_out": bytes_out,
                    "total_packets": b["total_packets"],
                    "flow_count": b["flows"],
                    "direction": direction,
                    "sni": b["sni"],
                },
                first_seen=b["first_seen"],
                last_seen=b["last_seen"],
                mitre_techniques=mitre,
            ))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\lateral_movement.py
# ==========================================
"""
LateralMovementDetector вЂ” РїСЂРёР·РЅР°РєРё РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµР№ СЃРµС‚Рё.

РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»: РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ в†’ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ С‚СЂР°С„РёРє РЅР° РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ
(445 SMB, 135 RPC, 139 NetBIOS, 3389 RDP, 5985/5986 WinRM) РР›Р
HTTP-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹ Рє /wsman, /cimv2 РР›Р User-Agent РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ
(WinRM, PsExec, Impacket).

Confidence:
  - 0.8 вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р» UA-РјР°СЂРєРµСЂ РёР»Рё URI-РјР°СЂРєРµСЂ
  - 0.6 вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕСЂС‚, Рё С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРё >= N flows Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ
          (РѕРґРёРЅ RDP-СЃРµР°РЅСЃ Рє jump-host вЂ” РЅРѕСЂРјР°, РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flows вЂ”
          РїРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂ/СЃРєР°РЅ/Р»Р°С‚РµСЂР°Р»РєР°)

allowlist: config.lateral_movement_admin_srcs вЂ” jump-С…РѕСЃС‚С‹, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ
С…РѕРґСЏС‚ РЅР° СЌС‚Рё РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ РєР°Рє С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕР№ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹. РџСѓСЃС‚Рѕ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ.

РђРіСЂРµРіР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕ (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port). РќРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ flows Рє РѕРґРЅРѕРјСѓ
С…РѕСЃС‚Сѓ вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ finding.
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


@dataclass
class _Bucket:
    flow_count: int = 0
    user_agents: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    uris: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    match_reasons: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, ts: float | None) -> None:
        if ts is None:
            return
        if self.first_seen is None or ts < self.first_seen:
            self.first_seen = ts
        if self.last_seen is None or ts > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = ts


def _match_ua(ua: str | None, markers: frozenset[str]) -> str | None:
    if not ua:
        return None
    ua_lc = ua.lower()
    for m in markers:
        if m.lower() in ua_lc:
            return m
    return None


def _match_uri(uri: str | None, markers: frozenset[str]) -> str | None:
    if not uri:
        return None
    uri_lc = uri.lower()
    for m in markers:
        if m.lower() in uri_lc:
            return m
    return None


class LateralMovementDetector:
    name = "lateral_movement"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        ports = config.lateral_movement_ports
        ua_markers = config.lateral_movement_ua_markers
        uri_markers = config.lateral_movement_uri_markers
        admin_srcs = config.lateral_movement_admin_srcs

        agg: dict[tuple[str, str, int], _Bucket] = defaultdict(_Bucket)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if key.dst_port not in ports:
                continue
            # src РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРј. РРЅР°С‡Рµ вЂ” РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚СЂР°С„РёРє
            # РёР· РёРЅС‚РµСЂРЅРµС‚Р° РЅР° РЅР°С€ SMB-РїРѕСЂС‚, СЌС‚Рѕ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»
            # (СЃРј. suspicious_port / port_scan).
            if not self._is_internal(key.src_ip, config):
                continue
            # allowlist: IP, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ Р»РµРіРёС‚РёРјРЅРѕ С…РѕРґСЏС‚ РЅР° СЌС‚Рё РїРѕСЂС‚С‹
            if key.src_ip in admin_srcs:
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            bucket.flow_count += 1

            ua_hit = _match_ua(flow.http_user_agent, ua_markers)
            if ua_hit:
                bucket.user_agents.add(flow.http_user_agent)
                bucket.match_reasons.add("user_agent")

            uri_hit = _match_uri(flow.http_uri, uri_markers)
            if uri_hit:
                bucket.uris.add(flow.http_uri)
                bucket.match_reasons.add("uri")

            bucket.touch(flow.first_ts)
            bucket.touch(flow.last_ts)

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_ip, dst_port), bucket in agg.items():
            # РџРѕСЂС‚ СЃР°Рј РїРѕ СЃРµР±Рµ С„Р»Р°РіСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРё РјРЅРѕР¶РµСЃС‚РІРµ flows.
            # РћРґРёРЅ RDP-СЃРµР°РЅСЃ Рє jump-host вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРѕСЂРјР°, РЅРµ finding.
            if not bucket.match_reasons:
                if bucket.flow_count < config.lateral_movement_min_flows_port_only:
                    continue
                bucket.match_reasons.add("port")

            if ("user_agent" in bucket.match_reasons
                    or "uri" in bucket.match_reasons):
                confidence = 0.8
            else:
                confidence = 0.6

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="lateral_movement",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "flow_count": bucket.flow_count,
                    "match_reasons": sorted(bucket.match_reasons),
                    "user_agents": sorted(bucket.user_agents)[:5],
                    "uris": sorted(bucket.uris)[:5],
                },
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings

    def _is_internal(self, ip: str, config: Config) -> bool:
        return any(ip.startswith(p) for p in config.private_networks)

    # РёРЅС‚РµСЂРїСЂРµС‚Р°С‚РѕСЂ (T1059). РћР±Рµ С‚РµС…РЅРёРєРё СѓРјРµСЃС‚РЅС‹.
