# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\nxdomain_burst.py
# ==========================================
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding

# v0.9.0: nxdomain_burst windowing


def _max_in_window(ts_sorted: list[float], window_sec: int) -> int:
    """
    РњР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј С‚РѕС‡РµРє РІ СЃРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РµРј РѕРєРЅРµ window_sec СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ.

    ts_sorted вЂ” РѕС‚СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕ РІРѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚Р°РЅРёСЋ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє unix-ts.
    O(n), РґРІР° СѓРєР°Р·Р°С‚РµР»СЏ: СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂСЏРµРј right, СЃРґРІРёРіР°РµРј left, РїРѕРєР°
    СЂР°Р·РЅРёС†Р° ts[right] - ts[left] РЅРµ РІР»РµР·РµС‚ РІ РѕРєРЅРѕ.
    """
    if not ts_sorted:
        return 0
    left = 0
    best = 1
    for right in range(len(ts_sorted)):
        while ts_sorted[right] - ts_sorted[left] > window_sec:
            left += 1
        best = max(best, right - left + 1)
    return best


class NXDOMAINBurstDetector:
    """
    РћРґРёРЅ С…РѕСЃС‚ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёР» >= N NXDOMAIN Р·Р° РѕРєРЅРѕ W СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ.

    v0.9.0: СЃРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РµРµ РѕРєРЅРѕ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ total count Р·Р° С„Р°Р№Р».

    Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ (0.7.0вЂ“0.8.0) СЃС‡РёС‚Р°Р»Рё Р·Р° РІРµСЃСЊ С„Р°Р№Р» вЂ” РЅР° FIN-2015
    РґР°РІР°Р»Рѕ 93 findings РёР· 316 (29%). Р‘РѕР»СЊС€РёРЅСЃС‚РІРѕ вЂ” СЂР°Р·РјР°Р·Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
    РїРѕ С‡Р°СЃСѓ РѕРїРµС‡Р°С‚РєРё РІ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂР°С… Рё NXDOMAIN-С‹ РѕС‚ Р»РµРіРёС‚РёРјРЅС‹С…
    СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃРѕРІ.

    Backward compat: РµСЃР»Рё РґР»СЏ src РЅРµС‚ timestamps (СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ С‚РµСЃС‚С‹,
    СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕР№ state), РїР°РґР°РµРј РЅР° total count Рё РїРѕРјРµС‡Р°РµРј
    evidence windowed=False.
    """
    name = "nxdomain_burst"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []
        threshold = config.nxdomain_burst_threshold
        window = config.nxdomain_burst_window_sec
        min_ratio = config.nxdomain_burst_min_ratio

        for src_ip, total in state.dns_nxdomain_by_src.items():
            if total == 0:
                continue

            ts_list = state.dns_nxdomain_ts_by_src.get(src_ip)
            if ts_list:
                ts_sorted = sorted(ts_list)
                max_win = _max_in_window(ts_sorted, window)
                windowed = True
                signal = max_win
            else:
                max_win = total
                windowed = False
                signal = total

            if signal < threshold:
                continue

            # v0.9.0: ratio-С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ. РЎРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІСЃРµРіРѕ DNS-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ РѕС‚
            # СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ src (РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЏ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅС‹Рµ). Р•СЃР»Рё РґРѕР»СЏ NXDOMAIN РЅРёР·РєР° вЂ”
            # СЌС‚Рѕ С€СѓРј РѕС‚ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂР° СЃ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРµР№.
            all_q = state.dns_queries_by_src.get(src_ip)
            total_dns = sum(all_q.values()) if all_q else signal
            ratio = signal / total_dns if total_dns > 0 else 1.0

            if ratio < min_ratio:
                continue

            confidence = round(min(0.9, 0.4 + signal * 0.05), 3)

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="nxdomain_burst",
                value=src_ip,
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "nxdomain_count": total,
                    "max_in_window": max_win,
                    "window_sec": window,
                    "threshold": threshold,
                    "windowed": windowed,
                    "ratio": round(ratio, 3),
                    "total_dns_queries": total_dns,
                },
            ))

        return findings



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\port_scan.py
# ==========================================
"""
PortScanDetector вЂ” РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ СЃРєР°РЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ: РѕРґРёРЅ src_ip, РјРЅРѕРіРѕ dst_ip,
РѕРґРёРЅ dst_port.

РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»: src_ip РѕР±СЂР°С‚РёР»СЃСЏ Рє >= N СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј dst_ip РЅР° РѕРґРёРЅ dst_port, РїСЂРёС‡С‘Рј
Рє РєР°Р¶РґРѕРјСѓ вЂ” РЅРµ Р±РѕР»РµРµ M flow'РѕРІ (СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ 1 SYN РЅР° С†РµР»СЊ).

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ suspicious_port:
  - suspicious_port  в†’ finding per (dst_ip, dst_port), РјРЅРѕРіРѕ findings
  - port_scan        в†’ finding per (src_ip, dst_port), РѕРґРёРЅ finding
                       СЃ evidence: distinct_dsts, top_dsts

РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ brute_force:
  - brute_force в†’ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flow'РѕРІ Рє РћР”РќРћРњРЈ dst_ip
  - port_scan   в†’ РїРѕ 1-2 flow Рє РљРђР–Р”РћРњРЈ РёР· РјРЅРѕРіРёС… dst_ip

Р•СЃР»Рё Сѓ src_ip РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flow'РѕРІ РєРѕ РјРЅРѕРіРёРј dst_ip вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РІ РѕСЃРЅРѕРІРЅРѕРј Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ
СЃ РїР°СЂР°Р»Р»РµР»СЊРЅС‹РјРё СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏРјРё. РћС‚СЃРµРёРІР°РµРј С‡РµСЂРµР· max_flows_per_dst.

РџРѕСЂС‚С‹ 80/443 РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅС‹ РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ: СЃРєР°РЅ РЅР° РЅРёС… РЅРµРѕС‚Р»РёС‡РёРј РѕС‚ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂР°
(675 СЃР°Р№С‚РѕРІ Р·Р° РґРµРЅСЊ вЂ” РЅРѕСЂРјР°), Р° rate/flow_count СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ С‚Р°Рј Р±РµСЃРїРѕР»РµР·РЅС‹.
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class PortScanDetector:
    name = "port_scan"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        # (src_ip, dst_port) в†’ Counter[dst_ip]
        scans: dict[tuple[str, int], Counter] = defaultdict(Counter)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.proto != "tcp":
                continue
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            scans[(key.src_ip, key.dst_port)][key.dst_ip] += 1

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (src_ip, dst_port), dst_counts in scans.items():
            # 80/443 вЂ” Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ, РЅРµ СЃРєР°РЅ. РЎРј. docstring.
            if dst_port in config.port_scan_excluded_ports:
                continue

            distinct_dsts = len(dst_counts)
            if distinct_dsts < config.port_scan_min_distinct_dsts:
                continue

            # Р•СЃР»Рё Рє Р±РѕР»СЊС€РёРЅСЃС‚РІСѓ С†РµР»РµР№ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ flow'РѕРІ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ СЃРєР°РЅ.
            # РЎС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РґРѕР»СЋ В«С‚РѕРЅРєРёС…В» С†РµР»РµР№ (в‰¤ max_flows_per_dst).
            thin = sum(
                1 for c in dst_counts.values()
                if c <= config.port_scan_max_flows_per_dst
            )
            if thin / distinct_dsts < 0.7:
                continue

            total_flows = sum(dst_counts.values())
            top_dsts = [
                {"ip": ip, "flows": c}
                for ip, c in dst_counts.most_common(10)
            ]

            confidence = 0.9 if distinct_dsts >= 30 else 0.75
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="port_scan",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> *:{dst_port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "distinct_dsts": distinct_dsts,
                    "total_flows": total_flows,
                    "thin_targets": thin,
                    "top_dsts": top_dsts,
                    "threshold": config.port_scan_min_distinct_dsts,
                },
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\smtp_spam.py
# ==========================================
"""
SMTP spam РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ.

РџСЂРёР·РЅР°Рє РјР°СЃСЃРѕРІРѕР№ СЂР°СЃСЃС‹Р»РєРё: СЃ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… RCPT TO.
РўРёРїРёС‡РЅРѕ РґР»СЏ РєРѕРјРїСЂРѕРјРµС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ С…РѕСЃС‚Р°-СЃРїР°РјР±РѕС‚Р° РёР»Рё newsletter-
СЃРµСЂРІРёСЃР° (РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРµ вЂ” FP, РЅРѕ СЂРµР¶Рµ РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ РєРѕСЂРїРѕСЂР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕРј
pcap, С‡РµРј РїРµСЂРІРѕРµ).

Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РёР· state.smtp_rcpt_by_src Рё state.smtp_mailer_by_src
(Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚ SMTPExtractor).

Confidence РїРѕ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІСѓ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°С‚РµР»РµР№:
  50вЂ“199      0.7
  200вЂ“999     0.8
  1000+       0.9

Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»: РµСЃР»Рё X-Mailer/UA СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ РјР°СЃСЃ-РјРµР№Р»РµСЂРЅС‹Рµ
РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹ вЂ” РїРёС€РµРј РІ evidence. РќР° confidence РЅРµ РІР»РёСЏРµС‚: РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹
СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РѕР±С‰РёРµ (PHPMailer РµСЃС‚СЊ Сѓ РїРѕР»РѕРІРёРЅС‹ CMS).
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


# РџРѕРґСЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РІ X-Mailer / User-Agent, С‡Р°СЃС‚С‹Рµ Сѓ РјР°СЃСЃ-РјРµР№Р»РµСЂРѕРІ.
# РќР° confidence РЅРµ РІР»РёСЏСЋС‚ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІ evidence РґР»СЏ Р°РЅР°Р»РёС‚РёРєР°.
_MASS_MAILER_MARKERS = (
    "phpmailer",
    "swiftmailer",
    "mass",
    "bulk",
    "sender",
    "mailer",
    "bot",
)


class SMTP_SpamDetector:
    """
    SMTP СЃРїР°Рј: 50+ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… RCPT TO СЃ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src.

    РќРµ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµС‚ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РїРёСЃРµРј (РЅРµС‚ body) вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ envelope.
    Р•СЃР»Рё РІ evidence РїРѕРїР°РґР°РµС‚ 200+ РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°С‚РµР»РµР№ СЃ РѕРґРЅРёРј MAIL FROM вЂ”
    СЌС‚Рѕ СЃ Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№ РІРµСЂРѕСЏС‚РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ СЃРїР°Рј-Р±РѕС‚.
    """
    name = "smtp_spam"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []
        threshold = config.smtp_spam_min_recipients

        for src_ip, rcpt_set in state.smtp_rcpt_by_src.items():
            count = len(rcpt_set)
            if count < threshold:
                continue

            if count >= 1000:
                confidence = 0.9
            elif count >= 200:
                confidence = 0.8
            else:
                confidence = 0.7

            # РС‰РµРј РјР°СЃСЃ-РјРµР№Р»РµСЂРЅС‹Рµ РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹.
            mailers = state.smtp_mailer_by_src.get(src_ip, set())
            matched_markers: list[str] = []
            for m in mailers:
                low = m.lower()
                for marker in _MASS_MAILER_MARKERS:
                    if marker in low and marker not in matched_markers:
                        matched_markers.append(marker)

            # HELO вЂ” РґР»СЏ evidence.
            helo = state.smtp_helo_by_src.get(src_ip, "")

            # FROM-Р°РґСЂРµСЃР° вЂ” Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РѕРґРёРЅ (РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ РёС… РЅРµРјРЅРѕРіРѕ).
            from_addrs = sorted(state.smtp_from_by_src.get(src_ip, set()))
            from_sample = from_addrs[0] if from_addrs else ""

            evidence = {
                "src_ip": src_ip,
                "recipients_count": count,
                "threshold": threshold,
                "helo": helo,
                "from_sample": from_sample,
                "mailer_markers": sorted(matched_markers),
            }

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="smtp_spam",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {count} recipients",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence=evidence,
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\sni_mismatch.py
# ==========================================
from collections import defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class SNIMismatchDetector:
    """
    TLS-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ, С‡РµР№ SNI РЅРµ РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡Р°Р»СЃСЏ РІ DNS-РћРўР’Р•РўРђРҐ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ Р·Р°С…РІР°С‚Р°.

    РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёРµ РѕС‚ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕР№ Р»РѕРіРёРєРё (v0.7.0): СЃСЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РµРј СЃ dns_answers_by_name
    (РѕС‚РІРµС‚С‹), Р° РЅРµ dns_queries (Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹). Р§С‚Рѕ СЌС‚Рѕ РґР°С‘С‚:
      - DoH: РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ СЃРїСЂРѕСЃРёР» example.com, РЅРѕ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ С€С‘Р» С‡РµСЂРµР· DoH-РєР°РЅР°Р»
        (РІ plaintext DNS РЅРµ РІРёРґРµРЅ). РЎС‚Р°СЂРѕРµ РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рѕ вЂ” FP, РЅРѕРІРѕРµ РјРѕР»С‡РёС‚.
      - NXDOMAIN: Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ Р±С‹Р», РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° РЅРµС‚ в†’ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРѕ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»РёРј.
      - CNAME: РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ СЂРµР·РѕР»РІРёР» www.foo.com в†’ cdn.foo.com. РџСЂР°РІРёР»Рѕ
        СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚ cdn.foo.com СЂРµР·РѕР»РІРЅСѓС‚С‹Рј (РѕРЅ РІ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏС…), FP РЅРµС‚.

    Р§С‚Рѕ СЌС‚Рѕ РѕР·РЅР°С‡Р°РµС‚:
    - domain fronting (SNI в‰  СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С…РѕСЃС‚);
    - hardcoded IP/РґРѕРјРµРЅ РІ РјР°Р»РІР°СЂРё вЂ” СЂРµР·РѕР»РІРёС‚СЃСЏ РЅРµ С‡РµСЂРµР· Р»РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ DNS;
    - РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ СЃ РґРѕР»РіРёРј DNS-РєРµС€РµРј (СЂРµР·РѕР»РІ Р±С‹Р» РґРѕ Р·Р°С…РІР°С‚Р°).

    РђРіСЂРµРіР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕ SNI, Р° РЅРµ РїРѕ flow: 8 СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёР№ Рє РѕРґРЅРѕРјСѓ РґРѕРјРµРЅСѓ вЂ”
    РѕРґРёРЅ finding, Р° РЅРµ 8.

    Allowlist: sni_mismatch_allowlist (DoH/OCSP) +
    legitimate_brand_domains (google/microsoft/apple Рё РїСЂ.).
    """
    name = "sni_mismatch"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        # Р РµР·РѕР»РІ РїРѕ РћРўР’Р•РўРђРњ, РЅРµ РїРѕ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР°Рј. РЎРј. docstring.
        # resolved_names вЂ” РєР»СЋС‡Рё (РїСЂСЏРјРѕР№ A-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё).
        # resolved_values вЂ” Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ (CNAME-С‚Р°СЂРіРµС‚С‹ РІ С†РµРїРѕС‡РєРµ).
        resolved_names = set(state.dns_answers_by_name.keys())
        resolved_values: set[str] = set()
        for ips in state.dns_answers_by_name.values():
            resolved_values |= ips

        # РўСЂРё РЅР°Р±РѕСЂР°, СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРѕРІР°РЅРѕ СЃ extract_tls_host_facts
        # (РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рѕ SniWithoutDnsRule РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ С‚Рµ Р¶Рµ СЃР°РјС‹Рµ):
        #   - sni_mismatch_allowlist: DoH, OCSP, CRL
        #   - legitimate_brand_domains: google/apple/microsoft/twitter
        #   - beaconing_allowlist_suffixes: CDN (akamai, cloudflare Рё РїСЂ.)
        allowlist = (
            config.sni_mismatch_allowlist
            | config.legitimate_brand_domains
            | config.beaconing_allowlist_suffixes
        )

        # SNI в†’ Р°РіСЂРµРіР°С‚
        agg: dict[str, dict] = defaultdict(lambda: {
            "flow_count": 0,
            "dst_ips": set(),
            "dst_ports": set(),
            "total_bytes": 0,
            "first_seen": None,
            "last_seen": None,
        })

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            sni = flow.sni
            if not sni:
                continue
            if sni in resolved_names or sni in resolved_values:
                continue
            if self._in_allowlist(sni, allowlist):
                continue

            bucket = agg[sni]
            bucket["flow_count"] += 1
            bucket["dst_ips"].add(key.dst_ip)
            if key.dst_port is not None:
                bucket["dst_ports"].add(key.dst_port)
            bucket["total_bytes"] += flow.bytes_sent

            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if bucket["first_seen"] is None or flow.first_ts < bucket["first_seen"]:
                    bucket["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if bucket["last_seen"] is None or flow.last_ts > bucket["last_seen"]:
                    bucket["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for sni, bucket in agg.items():
            if bucket["flow_count"] < config.sni_mismatch_min_flows:
                continue

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="sni_without_dns",
                value=sni,
                confidence=0.6,
                evidence={
                    "sni": sni,
                    "flow_count": bucket["flow_count"],
                    "dst_ips": sorted(bucket["dst_ips"]),
                    "dst_ports": sorted(bucket["dst_ports"]),
                    "total_bytes": bucket["total_bytes"],
                },
                first_seen=bucket["first_seen"],
                last_seen=bucket["last_seen"],
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: f.value)
        return findings

    def _in_allowlist(self, sni: str, allowlist: set[str]) -> bool:
        """РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РЅР° РІС…РѕР¶РґРµРЅРёРµ РІ allowlist РїРѕ СЃСѓС„С„РёРєСЃСѓ.

        `smartscreen.microsoft.com` РїРѕРєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ Рё
        `nav-edge.smartscreen.microsoft.com`. РўРѕС‡РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ вЂ” С‚РѕР¶Рµ
        СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚.
        """
        for entry in allowlist:
            if sni == entry or sni.endswith("." + entry):
                return True
        return False


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\ssh_brute_force.py
# ==========================================
"""
SSH brute force РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ.

РџРµСЂРµР±РѕСЂ РїР°СЂРѕР»РµР№ РїРѕ SSH: СЃ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ (dst_ip, dst_port)
РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёС‚ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ SSH-Р±Р°РЅРЅРµСЂРѕРІ. РћРґРёРЅ Р±Р°РЅРЅРµСЂ = РѕРґРЅР° СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЏ (RFC 4253).

Confidence РїРѕ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІСѓ:
  20-49 РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє вЂ” 0.75
  50-99         вЂ” 0.85
  100+          вЂ” 0.95

Severity: high (РёР· _SEVERITY_BY_TYPE РІ models.py).

Р§С‚Рѕ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµРј:
  - Р•РґРёРЅРёС‡РЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё (РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ SSH-СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё).
  - Р Р°Р·РЅС‹Рµ dst вЂ” Р»РµРіРёС‚РёРјРЅС‹Рµ РјРЅРѕР¶РµСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class SSHBruteForceDetector:
    """
    SSH brute force: 20+ Р±Р°РЅРЅРµСЂРѕРІ РѕС‚ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ src Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂРµ
    (dst_ip, dst_port). РђРЅР°Р»РѕРі ftp_brute_force, РЅРѕ С‡РµСЂРµР· SSH-РїРѕСЂС‚С‹.
    """
    name = "ssh_brute_force"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        findings: list[Finding] = []
        threshold = config.ssh_brute_force_min_flows

        for (src, dst, port), count in state.ssh_client_attempts.items():
            if count < threshold:
                continue

            if count >= 100:
                confidence = 0.95
            elif count >= 50:
                confidence = 0.85
            else:
                confidence = 0.75

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="ssh_brute_force",
                value=f"{src} -> {dst}:{port}",
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src,
                    "dst_ip": dst,
                    "dst_port": port,
                    "attempts": count,
                    "threshold": threshold,
                },
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\suspicious_port.py
# ==========================================
"""
SuspiciousPortDetector вЂ” СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ Рє РІРЅРµС€РЅРёРј Р°РґСЂРµСЃР°Рј РЅР° РїРѕСЂС‚С‹ РёР·
suspicious_ports.

РћСЃРѕР±РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё:
- РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РїСЂРёРІР°С‚РЅС‹Рµ РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ (10.x, 192.168.x, multicast Рё С‚.Рї.).
  РЎРёРіРЅР°Р» = В«РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ СЃС‚СѓС‡РёС‚СЃСЏ РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ РЅР° РЅРµСЃС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Р№ РїРѕСЂС‚В»,
  Р° РЅРµ Р»СЋР±РѕРµ СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЃ РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРј РёР· СЃРїРёСЃРєР°;
- Р°РіСЂРµРіРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІСЃРµ flow'С‹ РїРѕ (dst_ip, dst_port) РІ РѕРґРёРЅ finding,
  С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ 474 СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ Рє РѕРґРЅРѕРјСѓ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂСѓ РґР°РІР°Р»Рё РѕРґРЅСѓ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°,
  Р° РЅРµ 474.

Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ port_scan:
- РµСЃР»Рё РІСЃРµ src_ips РІ finding'Рµ вЂ” СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂС‹ РЅР° СЌС‚РѕРј РїРѕСЂС‚Сѓ
  (Сѓ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ в‰Ґ port_scan_min_distinct_dsts СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… dst РЅР° СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїРѕСЂС‚),
  С‚Рѕ finding РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ вЂ” РµРіРѕ РїРѕРєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ PortScanDetector.
- РµСЃР»Рё СЃСЂРµРґРё src_ips РµСЃС‚СЊ С…РѕС‚СЊ РѕРґРёРЅ РЅРµ-СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂ, finding РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ,
  С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏС‚СЊ Р»РµРіРёС‚РёРјРЅС‹Р№ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р».
"""
from collections import defaultdict

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


class SuspiciousPortDetector:
    name = "suspicious_port"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        # РєР»СЋС‡: (dst_ip, dst_port) в†’ Р°РіСЂРµРіР°С‚
        agg: dict[tuple[str, int], dict] = defaultdict(lambda: {
            "flow_count": 0,
            "src_ips": set(),
            "total_bytes": 0,
            "first_seen": None,
            "last_seen": None,
        })

        # Р”Р»СЏ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР° port_scan: (src_ip, dst_port) в†’ set[dst_ip].
        # Р•СЃР»Рё Сѓ РїР°СЂС‹ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… dst вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂ, Рё РµРіРѕ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ
        # suspicious_port findings РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂСѓСЋС‚ port_scan.
        src_dst_map: dict[tuple[str, int], set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            if key.dst_port is None:
                continue
            if key.dst_port not in config.suspicious_ports:
                continue
            if self._is_private(key.dst_ip, config):
                continue

            bucket = agg[(key.dst_ip, key.dst_port)]
            bucket["flow_count"] += 1
            bucket["src_ips"].add(key.src_ip)
            bucket["total_bytes"] += flow.bytes_sent

            if flow.first_ts is not None:
                if (bucket["first_seen"] is None
                        or flow.first_ts < bucket["first_seen"]):
                    bucket["first_seen"] = flow.first_ts
            if flow.last_ts is not None:
                if (bucket["last_seen"] is None
                        or flow.last_ts > bucket["last_seen"]):
                    bucket["last_seen"] = flow.last_ts

            src_dst_map[(key.src_ip, key.dst_port)].add(key.dst_ip)

        # РџР°СЂС‹ (src_ip, dst_port), РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СѓР¶Рµ РїРѕРєСЂС‹С‚С‹ port_scan'РѕРј.
        scan_pairs: set[tuple[str, int]] = {
            (src, port)
            for (src, port), dsts in src_dst_map.items()
            if len(dsts) >= config.port_scan_min_distinct_dsts
        }

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for (dst_ip, dst_port), bucket in agg.items():
            srcs = bucket["src_ips"]

            # Р•СЃР»Рё РІСЃРµ src РІ СЌС‚РѕРј finding'Рµ вЂ” СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂС‹ РЅР° СЌС‚РѕРј РїРѕСЂС‚Сѓ,
            # finding РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂСѓРµС‚ port_scan. РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј.
            if srcs and all(
                (s, dst_port) in scan_pairs for s in srcs
            ):
                continue

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="suspicious_port",
                value=f"{dst_ip}:{dst_port}",
                confidence=0.5,
                evidence={
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "dst_port": dst_port,
                    "flow_count": bucket["flow_count"],
                    "src_ips": sorted(bucket["src_ips"]),
                    "total_bytes": bucket["total_bytes"],
                },
                first_seen=bucket["first_seen"],
                last_seen=bucket["last_seen"],
            ))

        # СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° вЂ” РґР»СЏ РґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёР·РјР°
        findings.sort(key=lambda f: f.value)
        return findings

    def _is_private(self, ip: str, config: Config) -> bool:
        return any(ip.startswith(prefix) for prefix in config.private_networks)


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\typosquatting.py
# ==========================================
"""
TyposquattingDetector вЂ” РїРѕРґРґРµР»РєРё РїРѕРґ РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ Р±СЂРµРЅРґС‹.

Р›РѕРіРёРєР°:
  - contains:    Р±СЂРµРЅРґ вЂ” РїРѕРґСЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° РІ SLD (event-datamicrosoft.live)
  - levenshtein: SLD Р±Р»РёР·РѕРє Рє Р±СЂРµРЅРґСѓ (micr0soft.com)

РљР»СЋС‡РµРІРѕРµ: РјР°С‚С‡РёРЅРі РёРґС‘С‚ РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ РїРѕ SLD (registered domain,
РІС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ СЃРїСЂР°РІР°). РџРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ:
  googleads.g.doubleclick.net -> SLD doubleclick, РЅРµ google
  app.loadimpact.com          -> SLD loadimpact, РЅРµ app

РўРѕС‡РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ SLD СЃ Р±СЂРµРЅРґРѕРј (google.com) вЂ” РЅРµ typosquat.

Levenshtein РґРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅ РґР»РёРЅРѕР№:
  - SLD < 5 СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ РЅРµ СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ (app/web/mail вЂ” РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ РёРјРµРЅР°)
  - SLD РєРѕСЂРѕС‡Рµ Р±СЂРµРЅРґР° РЅР° 2+ вЂ” РЅРµ СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ

v0.9.0: РґРІСѓС…СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРІС‹Рµ TLD (co.uk, com.au, co.jp, ...) РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶Р°РЅС‹
С‡РµСЂРµР· domain_utils. bbc.co.uk в†’ SLD "bbc", РЅРµ "co". РџРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ Public
Suffix List РЅРµ С‚СЏРЅРµРј вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РєРµР№СЃС‹.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


def levenshtein(a: str, b: str) -> int:
    if a == b:
        return 0
    if not a:
        return len(b)
    if not b:
        return len(a)
    prev = list(range(len(b) + 1))
    for i, ca in enumerate(a, 1):
        cur = [i]
        for j, cb in enumerate(b, 1):
            cur.append(min(
                cur[j - 1] + 1,
                prev[j] + 1,
                prev[j - 1] + (ca != cb),
            ))
        prev = cur
    return prev[-1]


def _registered_label(domain: str) -> str:
    """
    v0.9.0: SLD Р±РµР· РїРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ Рё TLD СЃ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРѕР№ РґРІСѓС…СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРІС‹С… TLD.

    googleads.g.doubleclick.net -> doubleclick
    a.b.foo.co.uk              -> foo
    """
    from paradox.domain_utils import registered_label
    return registered_label(domain)


def _check_squat(sld: str, brand: str, lev_threshold: int) -> str | None:
    """Р’РµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ kind ('contains'/'levenshtein') РµСЃР»Рё SLD вЂ” РїРѕРґРґРµР»РєР° Р±СЂРµРЅРґР°."""
    if not sld or not brand:
        return None
    if "." in brand:                # goo.gl, aws.amazon.com вЂ” РЅРµ label-Р±СЂРµРЅРґС‹
        return None
    if sld == brand:                # СЃР°Рј Р±СЂРµРЅРґ (google.se -> SLD google)
        return None

    # --- contains ---
    # brand РІ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ SLD вЂ” sub-brand (microsoftstore) вЂ” РЅРµ typosquat.
    # brand РІ РєРѕРЅС†Рµ СЃ РѕС‡РµРЅСЊ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёРј РїСЂРµС„РёРєСЃРѕРј (s-microsoft, i-paypal)
    # вЂ” С‚СЂРµРєРёРЅРіРѕРІС‹Рµ РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ РєРѕСЂРїРѕСЂР°С†РёР№ вЂ” РЅРµ typosquat.
    # Р’СЃС‘ РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ (event-datamicrosoft) вЂ” contains-typosquat.
    if brand in sld:
        pos = sld.find(brand)
        if pos == 0:
            pass
        elif pos + len(brand) == len(sld) and pos <= 3:
            pass
        else:
            return "contains"

    # --- levenshtein ---
    if len(sld) < 5:
        return None
    if len(sld) < len(brand) - 1:
        return None
    if sld[0] != brand[0] or sld[-1] != brand[-1]:
        return None
    lev = levenshtein(sld, brand)
    if lev > lev_threshold:
        return None
    if lev / max(len(sld), len(brand)) > 0.25:
        return None
    return "levenshtein"


class TyposquattingDetector:
    name = "typosquatting"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        brands = config.protected_brands
        allowlist = config.legitimate_brand_domains
        lev_threshold = config.typosquatting_levenshtein_threshold

        findings: list[Finding] = []
        for qname, count in state.dns_queries.items():
            domain = qname.lower().strip(".")
            if not domain:
                continue

            # Allowlist: СЃР°Рј РґРѕРјРµРЅ РёР»Рё РµРіРѕ РїРѕРґРґРѕРјРµРЅ
            if any(domain == a or domain.endswith("." + a) for a in allowlist):
                continue

            sld = _registered_label(domain)
            if not sld:
                continue

            match: tuple[str, str] | None = None
            for brand in brands:
                kind = _check_squat(sld, brand, lev_threshold)
                if kind:
                    match = (brand, kind)
                    break

            if not match:
                continue

            brand, kind = match
            confidence = 0.75 if kind == "levenshtein" else 0.65
            findings.append(Finding(
                type="typosquatting",
                value=domain,
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence={
                    "brand": brand,
                    "kind": kind,
                    "label": sld,
                    "queries": count,
                },
            ))

        findings.sort(key=lambda f: (-f.confidence, f.value))
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\vulnerable_path.py
# ==========================================
"""
VulnerablePathDetector вЂ” РѕР±СЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ Рє СѓСЏР·РІРёРјС‹Рј РїСѓС‚СЏРј РІ HTTP Рё СЃРєР°С‡РёРІР°РЅРёРµ
РїРѕС‚РµРЅС†РёР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹С… С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ.

Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕРІРµСЂС… FlowStats.http_uri (Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ HTTPExtractor).

Р”РІР° СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р°:
  - vulnerable_path: URI СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ СѓСЏР·РІРёРјС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ
  - script_download: URI Р·Р°РєР°РЅС‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° РїРѕРґРѕР·СЂРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРёРµ

Р”РІРµ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё Р°РіСЂРµРіР°С†РёРё (Р·Р°РІРёСЃСЏС‚ РѕС‚ С‚РѕРіРѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ src):

  1. Individual вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё src РѕР±СЂР°С‚РёР»СЃСЏ Рє < N СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј URI Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С†РµР»Рё.
     РљР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ URI в†’ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ finding. Р­С‚Рѕ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РѕРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅРѕРµ
     СЃРєР°С‡РёРІР°РЅРёРµ РёР»Рё РµРґРёРЅРёС‡РЅР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° /wp-login.php.

  2. Web scanner вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё src РѕР±СЂР°С‚РёР»СЃСЏ Рє >= N СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј URI Рє РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С†РµР»Рё.
     Р­С‚Рѕ РЅРµ N Р°С‚Р°Рє, СЌС‚Рѕ РѕРґРёРЅ СЃРєР°РЅРµСЂ. РћРґРёРЅ finding web_scanner
     СЃ С‚СЂРµРјСЏ РґРµСЃСЏС‚РєР°РјРё РїСѓС‚РµР№ РІ evidence.

РўР°Рє 290 URI РѕС‚ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ IP РїСЂРµРІСЂР°С‰Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ 1 finding, Р° РЅРµ 290.
РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РѕРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅС‹Рµ РѕР±СЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ (РµРґРёРЅРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ /wp-login.php) РѕСЃС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ
РєР°Рє individual findings.

Confidence:
  - vulnerable_path individual: 0.5 / 0.7 / 0.85 (РїРѕ count)
  - script_download individual: 0.65 / 0.8 / 0.9 (РїРѕ distinct srcs)
  - web_scanner: 0.9 (РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґРµСЃСЏС‚РєРѕРІ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… URI вЂ” РїРѕС‡С‚Рё
    РЅР°РІРµСЂРЅСЏРєР° Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРјРµРЅС‚)
"""
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, Finding


_EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE = 5


@dataclass
class _UriBucket:
    """РђРіСЂРµРіР°С‚ flows РїРѕ РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРјСѓ РїСѓС‚Рё РІ СЂР°РјРєР°С… (src, dst)."""
    kind: str
    path_key: str
    count: int = 0
    src_ips: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter)
    dst_ips: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    dst_ports: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)
    hosts: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    methods: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    user_agents: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    uri_samples: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    matched_paths: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    extension: str | None = None
    first_seen: float | None = None
    last_seen: float | None = None

    def touch(self, first_ts: float | None, last_ts: float | None) -> None:
        if first_ts is not None:
            if self.first_seen is None or first_ts < self.first_seen:
                self.first_seen = first_ts
        if last_ts is not None:
            if self.last_seen is None or last_ts > self.last_seen:
                self.last_seen = last_ts


def _confidence_vulnerable_path(count: int, distinct_srcs: int) -> float:
    if count >= 100 or distinct_srcs >= 10:
        return 0.85
    if count >= 10 or distinct_srcs >= 3:
        return 0.7
    return 0.5


def _confidence_script_download(count: int, distinct_srcs: int) -> float:
    if distinct_srcs >= 5:
        return 0.9
    if distinct_srcs >= 2:
        return 0.8
    if count >= 3:
        return 0.75
    return 0.65


class VulnerablePathDetector:
    name = "vulnerable_path"

    def run(self, state: CaptureState, config: Config) -> list[Finding]:
        vulnerable_paths = config.vulnerable_paths
        script_exts = config.suspicious_download_extensions
        scanner_threshold = config.web_scanner_min_distinct_paths

        # (kind, path_key) в†’ bucket РґР»СЏ individual findings
        per_uri: dict[tuple[str, str], _UriBucket] = {}

        # (src_ip, dst_ip) в†’ set[path_key] РґР»СЏ РґРµС‚РµРєС†РёРё scanner
        pairs: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        pair_ts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"first": None, "last": None, "count": 0,
                     "src_ports": set(), "hosts": set(),
                     "methods": set(), "user_agents": set()}
        )

        # (src_ip, dst_ip) в†’ kind в†’ list[(path, count)] РґР»СЏ scanner finding
        pair_paths: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, list[tuple[str, int]]]] = (
            defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
        )

        for key, flow in state.flows.items():
            uri = flow.http_uri
            if not uri:
                continue

            try:
                parsed = urlsplit(uri)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            raw_path = parsed.path or ""
            if not raw_path:
                continue
            path_key = unquote(raw_path).lower()

            matched_paths: list[str] = [
                vp for vp in vulnerable_paths if vp.lower() in path_key
            ]
            ext_matched: str | None = None
            for ext in script_exts:
                if path_key.endswith(ext):
                    ext_matched = ext
                    break

            kinds: list[str] = []
            if matched_paths:
                kinds.append("vulnerable_path")
            if ext_matched:
                kinds.append("script_download")
            if not kinds:
                continue

            pair_key = (key.src_ip, key.dst_ip)
            pairs[pair_key].add(path_key)

            pt = pair_ts[pair_key]
            pt["count"] += 1
            if key.src_port is not None:
                pt["src_ports"].add(key.src_port)
            if flow.http_host:
                pt["hosts"].add(flow.http_host)
            if flow.http_method:
                pt["methods"].add(flow.http_method)
            if flow.http_user_agent:
                pt["user_agents"].add(flow.http_user_agent)
            ts = flow.first_ts
            if ts is not None:
                if pt["first"] is None or ts < pt["first"]:
                    pt["first"] = ts
                if pt["last"] is None or ts > pt["last"]:
                    pt["last"] = ts

            for kind in kinds:
                pair_paths[pair_key][kind].append((path_key, 1))

                agg_key = (kind, path_key)
                bucket = per_uri.get(agg_key)
                if bucket is None:
                    bucket = _UriBucket(kind=kind, path_key=path_key)
                    per_uri[agg_key] = bucket

                bucket.count += 1
                bucket.src_ips[key.src_ip] += 1

                if len(bucket.dst_ips) < _EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE:
                    bucket.dst_ips.add(key.dst_ip)
                if (key.dst_port is not None
                        and len(bucket.dst_ports) < _EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE):
                    bucket.dst_ports.add(key.dst_port)
                if (flow.http_host
                        and len(bucket.hosts) < _EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE):
                    bucket.hosts.add(flow.http_host)
                if flow.http_method:
                    bucket.methods.add(flow.http_method)
                if (flow.http_user_agent
                        and len(bucket.user_agents) < _EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE):
                    bucket.user_agents.add(flow.http_user_agent)
                if len(bucket.uri_samples) < _EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE:
                    bucket.uri_samples.add(uri)

                if kind == "vulnerable_path":
                    for vp in matched_paths:
                        bucket.matched_paths.add(vp)
                else:
                    if bucket.extension is None:
                        bucket.extension = ext_matched

                bucket.touch(flow.first_ts, flow.last_ts)

        # --- Р¤Р°Р·Р° 1: РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј scanner-РїР°СЂС‹ ---
        scanner_pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
        for pair_key, paths in pairs.items():
            if len(paths) >= scanner_threshold:
                scanner_pairs.add(pair_key)

        # --- Р¤Р°Р·Р° 2: individual findings (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РЅРµ-scanner РїР°СЂ) ---
        findings: list[Finding] = []
        suppressed_pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()

        for (kind, path_key), bucket in per_uri.items():
            # Р•СЃР»Рё РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РѕР№ path_key Р’РЎР• src-РїР°СЂС‹ вЂ” scanner, РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј.
            # Р•СЃР»Рё С…РѕС‚СЊ РѕРґРЅР° РїР°СЂР° РЅРµ scanner вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј individual.
            src_ips_in_bucket = set(bucket.src_ips.keys())
            dst_ips_in_bucket = bucket.dst_ips
            still_relevant = False
            for src in src_ips_in_bucket:
                for dst in dst_ips_in_bucket:
                    if (src, dst) not in scanner_pairs:
                        still_relevant = True
                        break
                if still_relevant:
                    break
            if not still_relevant:
                continue

            distinct_srcs = len(bucket.src_ips)
            evidence = {
                "path": path_key,
                "uri_samples": sorted(bucket.uri_samples),
                "count": bucket.count,
                "distinct_src_ips": distinct_srcs,
                "top_src_ips": [
                    {"ip": ip, "count": c}
                    for ip, c in bucket.src_ips.most_common(_EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE)
                ],
                "dst_ips": sorted(bucket.dst_ips),
                "dst_ports": sorted(bucket.dst_ports),
                "hosts": sorted(bucket.hosts),
                "methods": sorted(bucket.methods),
                "user_agents": sorted(bucket.user_agents),
            }

            if kind == "vulnerable_path":
                evidence["matched_paths"] = [
                    {"path": p, "description": vulnerable_paths[p]}
                    for p in sorted(bucket.matched_paths)
                ]
                confidence = _confidence_vulnerable_path(
                    bucket.count, distinct_srcs,
                )
            else:
                evidence["extension"] = bucket.extension
                confidence = _confidence_script_download(
                    bucket.count, distinct_srcs,
                )

            findings.append(Finding(
                type=kind,
                value=path_key,
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence=evidence,
                first_seen=bucket.first_seen,
                last_seen=bucket.last_seen,
            ))

        # --- Р¤Р°Р·Р° 3: web_scanner findings ---
        for pair_key in scanner_pairs:
            src_ip, dst_ip = pair_key
            pt = pair_ts[pair_key]

            kind_paths: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
            for kind, lst in pair_paths[pair_key].items():
                # СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ path_key
                seen: dict[str, int] = {}
                for path_key, _ in lst:
                    seen[path_key] = seen.get(path_key, 0) + 1
                kind_paths[kind] = seen

            distinct_paths = len(pairs[pair_key])
            # РўРѕРї-10 РїСѓС‚РµР№ РїРѕ count
            all_paths: Counter = Counter()
            for d in kind_paths.values():
                for path_key, c in d.items():
                    all_paths[path_key] += c

            findings.append(Finding(
                type="web_scanner",
                value=f"{src_ip} -> {dst_ip}",
                confidence=0.9,
                evidence={
                    "src_ip": src_ip,
                    "dst_ip": dst_ip,
                    "distinct_paths": distinct_paths,
                    "total_requests": pt["count"],
                    "top_paths": [
                        {"path": p, "count": c}
                        for p, c in all_paths.most_common(20)
                    ],
                    "hosts": sorted(pt["hosts"])[:_EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE],
                    "methods": sorted(pt["methods"]),
                    "user_agents": sorted(pt["user_agents"])[:_EVIDENCE_SAMPLE_SIZE],
                    "threshold": scanner_threshold,
                },
                first_seen=pt["first"],
                last_seen=pt["last"],
            ))

        findings.sort(
            key=lambda f: (f.type, -f.evidence.get("count",
                          f.evidence.get("total_requests", 0)),
                          f.value)
        )
        return findings


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\detectors\yara_detector.py
# ==========================================
"""YARA-РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ РґР»СЏ РІС‹СЂРµР·Р°РЅРЅС‹С… С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ.

РЎРєР°РЅРёСЂСѓРµС‚ state.carved_files РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°РјРё YARA.
Р•СЃР»Рё Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєР° yara-python РЅРµ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР°, РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import logging

from paradox.models import Finding

try:
    import yara
except ImportError:
    yara = None

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class YARADetector:
    name = "yara"

    def run(self, state, config) -> list[Finding]:
        if yara is None:
            logger.debug("yara-python not installed, skipping YARA scan")
            return []

        rules_path = getattr(config, "yara_rules_path", None)
        if not rules_path or not os.path.exists(rules_path):
            # РџС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ РЅР°Р№С‚Рё rules/default.yar СЂСЏРґРѕРј СЃ РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚РѕРј
            default_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", "rules", "default.yar")
            if os.path.exists(default_path):
                rules_path = default_path
            else:
                return []

        try:
            rules = yara.compile(filepath=rules_path)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Failed to compile YARA rules: {e}")
            return []

        findings = []
        for cf in getattr(state, "carved_files", []):
            matches_by_rule = {}

            # 1. РЎРєР°РЅРёСЂСѓРµРј С„Р°Р№Р» РЅР° РґРёСЃРєРµ (РµСЃР»Рё РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ)
            if cf.saved_path and os.path.exists(cf.saved_path):
                try:
                    for m in rules.match(filepath=cf.saved_path):
                        matches_by_rule[m.rule] = m
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"YARA file match error ({cf.saved_path}): {e}")

            # 2. РЎРєР°РЅРёСЂСѓРµРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 64 Р±Р°Р№С‚Р° РёР· РїР°РјСЏС‚Рё (fallback)
            if cf.first_bytes_hex:
                try:
                    data_bytes = bytes.fromhex(cf.first_bytes_hex)
                    for m in rules.match(data=data_bytes):
                        # Р•СЃР»Рё РїСЂР°РІРёР»Рѕ СѓР¶Рµ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р»Рѕ РЅР° РїРѕР»РЅРѕРј С„Р°Р№Р»Рµ, РЅРµ РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂСѓРµРј
                        if m.rule not in matches_by_rule:
                            matches_by_rule[m.rule] = m
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"YARA data match error ({cf.sha256[:8]}): {e}")

            # 3. Р¤РѕСЂРјРёСЂСѓРµРј Finding
            for rule_name, m in matches_by_rule.items():
                findings.append(Finding(
                    type="yara_match",
                    value=rule_name,
                    confidence=0.9,
                    evidence={
                        "rule": m.rule,
                        "tags": list(m.tags),
                        "filename": cf.uri or cf.sha256[:12],
                        "sha256": cf.sha256,
                        "saved_path": cf.saved_path,
                    }
                ))
        return findings
