"""GreyNoise Community API вЂ” С€СѓРјРёС‚ Р»Рё IP.

https://api.greynoise.io/v3/community/{ip}
Р‘РµР· РєР»СЋС‡Р°: 50 Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ/РґРµРЅСЊ. РЎ РєР»СЋС‡РѕРј вЂ” Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ.
"""
from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class GreyNoiseEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "greynoise"
    supports = ("ip",)
    ttl_days = 1
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://api.greynoise.io/v3/community"
    timeout = 10.0

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        url = f"{self.base_url}/{ioc}"
        data, error = self._http_get_json(url)
        return self._resolve_not_found(data, error)



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\malwarebazaar.py
# ==========================================
"""MalwareBazaar (abuse.ch) вЂ” РѕР±СЂР°Р·С†С‹ malware РїРѕ SHA256.

РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ Auth-Key. POST form: query=get_info&hash=<sha256>
"""
import os

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class MalwareBazaarEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "malwarebazaar"
    supports = ("hash",)
    ttl_days = 30
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://mb-api.abuse.ch/api/v1/"
    timeout = 10.0

    def __init__(self, auth_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.auth_key = auth_key or os.environ.get("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY")

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.auth_key)

    def disabled_reason(self) -> str:
        return "ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY not set"

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        if ioc_type != "hash":
            return None, f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}"
        data, error = self._http_post_json(
            self.base_url,
            form={"query": "get_info", "hash": ioc},
            headers={"Auth-Key": self.auth_key or ""},
        )
        if error is None and isinstance(data, dict):
            if data.get("query_status") == "hash_not_found":
                return None, None
        return data, error



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\pipeline.py
# ==========================================
"""
Р’С‹СЃРѕРєРѕСѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРІР°СЏ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ.

Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ СЃ findings РІ РІРёРґРµ dict (С‚Рѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ run_pipeline).
Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµС‚ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚С‹ Finding вЂ” РґР»СЏ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІ Рё Р±СѓРґСѓС‰РёС…
РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№ РІ pipeline.

РџСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂРѕРІ РЅРµ РїСЂРёРєСЂРµРїР»СЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ Рє findings.
"""
from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.integrations.cache import EnrichmentCache
from paradox.integrations.enricher import (
    EnricherOrchestrator,
    collect_iocs_from_findings,
)


def build_default_orchestrator(config: Config) -> EnricherOrchestrator:
    """РЎРѕР±СЂР°С‚СЊ РѕСЂРєРµСЃС‚СЂР°С‚РѕСЂ РёР· REGISTRY, РѕС‚С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІР°РІ РїРѕ Config.

    РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂРѕРІ вЂ” РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё РІ REGISTRY, РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё.
    """
    from paradox.integrations.registry import REGISTRY

    enabled = set(config.enrichment_enabled_providers)
    providers = [
        cls() for name, cls in REGISTRY.items() if name in enabled
    ]
    cache = EnrichmentCache(config.enrichment_cache_path)
    return EnricherOrchestrator(providers, cache, config)


def attach_enrichment_to_findings(
    findings: list,
    enrichment_map: dict,
) -> None:
    """
    РџСЂРёРєСЂРµРїРёС‚СЊ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ РІ evidence["enrichment"].

    Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ Рё СЃ list[dict], Рё СЃ list[Finding].

    enrichment_map: {(ioc_type, ioc): [EnrichmentResult, ...]}
    РџСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕС€РёР±РєРё РёР»Рё РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ data) РЅРµ РїСЂРёРєСЂРµРїР»СЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ.
    """
    for f in findings:
        ev = _get_evidence(f)
        attached: dict = {}

        def _try_attach(ioc_type: str, ioc: str) -> None:
            if not isinstance(ioc, str) or not ioc:
                return
            results = enrichment_map.get((ioc_type, ioc))
            if not results:
                return
            per_ioc: dict = {}
            for r in results:
                if r.ok and r.data:
                    per_ioc[r.source] = r.data
            if per_ioc:
                attached[ioc] = per_ioc

        for field in ("dst_ip", "src_ip", "ip"):
            _try_attach("ip", ev.get(field))

        for field in ("base_domain", "sni", "host", "domain"):
            _try_attach("domain", ev.get(field))

        if attached:
            ev["enrichment"] = attached
            _set_evidence(f, ev)


def enrich_findings(findings: list, config: Config) -> dict:
    """
    РЎРѕР±СЂР°С‚СЊ IOC РёР· findings, РѕР±РѕРіР°С‚РёС‚СЊ, РїСЂРёРєСЂРµРїРёС‚СЊ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ.

    Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєСѓ:
      {
        "iocs_total": N,
        "iocs_enriched": M,
        "cache_hits": X,
        "cache_misses": Y,
        "rate_limited": Z,
        "provider_calls": K,
        "errors": E,
      }
    """
    iocs = collect_iocs_from_findings(findings)
    if not iocs:
        return {
            "iocs_total": 0,
            "iocs_enriched": 0,
            "cache_hits": 0,
            "cache_misses": 0,
            "rate_limited": 0,
            "provider_calls": 0,
            "errors": 0,
        }

    orch = build_default_orchestrator(config)
    try:
        enrichment_map = orch.enrich(iocs)
        attach_enrichment_to_findings(findings, enrichment_map)
        stats = dict(orch.stats)
        stats["iocs_total"] = len(iocs)
        stats["iocs_enriched"] = len(enrichment_map)
        return stats
    finally:
        orch.close()


# ---------- helpers: СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°СЋС‚ Рё СЃ dict, Рё СЃ Finding ----------

def _get_evidence(f) -> dict:
    """РџРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ evidence РєР°Рє dict (РёР· Finding РёР»Рё РёР· dict-finding)."""
    if isinstance(f, dict):
        ev = f.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
            ev = {}
            f["evidence"] = ev
        return ev
    # Finding (dataclass)
    if f.evidence is None:
        f.evidence = {}
    return f.evidence


def _set_evidence(f, ev: dict) -> None:
    """Р—Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ evidence РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ (РґР»СЏ dict вЂ” СѓР¶Рµ РјСѓС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ in-place)."""
    if not isinstance(f, dict):
        f.evidence = ev


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\rate_limit.py
# ==========================================
"""
Token bucket РґР»СЏ rate limit.

РЈ VT free tier: 4 Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° РІ РјРёРЅСѓС‚Сѓ. РЈ AbuseIPDB: 1000 РІ РґРµРЅСЊ.
РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕР№ token bucket вЂ” РµРґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РєР»Р°СЃСЃ РЅР° РІСЃРµ
Р»РёРјРёС‚С‹. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РїРµСЂРµРґ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рј Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРј, sleep РµСЃР»Рё РЅР°РґРѕ.
"""
import time


class TokenBucket:
    """
    Token bucket: N С‚РѕРєРµРЅРѕРІ, РІРѕСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёРµ M С‚РѕРєРµРЅРѕРІ РІ СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґСѓ.

    РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ:
      bucket = TokenBucket(capacity=4, refill_per_sec=4/60)
      # 4 Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° СЂР°Р·РѕРј, РїРѕС‚РѕРј РїРѕ РѕРґРЅРѕРјСѓ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ 15 СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ
    """

    def __init__(self, capacity: int, refill_per_sec: float):
        if capacity <= 0:
            raise ValueError("capacity must be > 0")
        if refill_per_sec <= 0:
            raise ValueError("refill_per_sec must be > 0")
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.refill_per_sec = refill_per_sec
        self._tokens = float(capacity)
        self._last_refill = time.monotonic()

    def _refill(self) -> None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - self._last_refill
        self._tokens = min(
            self.capacity,
            self._tokens + elapsed * self.refill_per_sec,
        )
        self._last_refill = now

    def try_acquire(self) -> bool:
        """РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±РѕРІР°С‚СЊ РІР·СЏС‚СЊ С‚РѕРєРµРЅ. РќРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚."""
        self._refill()
        if self._tokens >= 1:
            self._tokens -= 1
            return True
        return False

    def acquire(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> bool:
        """
        Р’Р·СЏС‚СЊ С‚РѕРєРµРЅ, Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓСЏ РґРѕ РїРѕСЏРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ.
        timeout вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РѕР¶РёРґР°РЅРёРµ РІ СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґР°С….
        Р’РµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ False, РµСЃР»Рё РЅРµ РґРѕР¶РґР°Р»РёСЃСЊ.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout if timeout else None
        while True:
            if self.try_acquire():
                return True
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                return False
            # Р¶РґС‘Рј РЅРµРјРЅРѕРіРѕ Рё РїСЂРѕР±СѓРµРј СЃРЅРѕРІР°
            wait = 1.0 / self.refill_per_sec
            time.sleep(min(wait, 0.5))


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\registry.py
# ==========================================
"""Р РµРµСЃС‚СЂ enrichment-РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂРѕРІ.

Р•РґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє РїСЂР°РІРґС‹: РєР°РєРѕР№ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ РїРѕРґ РєР°РєРёРј РёРјРµРЅРµРј.
pipeline.build_default_orchestrator Р±РµСЂС‘С‚ РѕС‚СЃСЋРґР°, С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµС‚ РїРѕ
config.enrichment_enabled_providers.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from paradox.integrations.abuseipdb import AbuseIPDBEnricher
from paradox.integrations.alienvault_otx import AlienVaultOTXEnricher
from paradox.integrations.greynoise import GreyNoiseEnricher
from paradox.integrations.malwarebazaar import MalwareBazaarEnricher
from paradox.integrations.shodan_internetdb import ShodanInternetDBEnricher
from paradox.integrations.threatfox import ThreatFoxEnricher
from paradox.integrations.urlhaus import URLhausEnricher
from paradox.integrations.urlscan import URLScanEnricher
from paradox.integrations.virustotal import VirusTotalEnricher

#: name -> РєР»Р°СЃСЃ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂР°. РРјРµРЅР° СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°СЋС‚ СЃ РєР»СЋС‡Р°РјРё
#: Config.enrichment_enabled_providers.
REGISTRY: dict[str, type] = {
    "shodan_internetdb": ShodanInternetDBEnricher,
    "abuseipdb": AbuseIPDBEnricher,
    "virustotal": VirusTotalEnricher,
    "greynoise": GreyNoiseEnricher,
    "alienvault_otx": AlienVaultOTXEnricher,
    "urlscan": URLScanEnricher,
    # РўСЂРµР±СѓСЋС‚ ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY вЂ” РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РІСЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ.
    "urlhaus": URLhausEnricher,
    "malwarebazaar": MalwareBazaarEnricher,
    "threatfox": ThreatFoxEnricher,
}


def get_provider(name: str):
    """РљР»Р°СЃСЃ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂР° РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё РёР»Рё None."""
    return REGISTRY.get(name)


def list_providers() -> list[str]:
    """РРјРµРЅР° РІСЃРµС… РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹С… РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂРѕРІ (РЅРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІРєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅРЅС‹С…)."""
    return sorted(REGISTRY.keys())



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\shodan_internetdb.py
# ==========================================
"""
Shodan InternetDB вЂ” Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ, Р±РµР· API-РєР»СЋС‡Р°.

GET https://internetdb.shodan.io/<ip>
Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚:
  {
    "ip": "8.8.8.8",
    "ports": [53, 443],
    "hostnames": ["dns.google"],
    "cpes": [],
    "vulns": ["CVE-2021-1234"],
    "tags": ["dns"]
  }

РћСЃРѕР±РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё:
- 404 = IP РЅРµ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ Shodan. Р­С‚Рѕ РЅРµ РѕС€РёР±РєР° вЂ” РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РЅРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С….
- РќРµС‚ РѕС„РёС†РёР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ rate limit, РЅРѕ РјС‹ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РёРІР°РµРј С‡РµСЂРµР· TokenBucket
  РІ РѕСЂРєРµСЃС‚СЂР°С‚РѕСЂРµ (РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ 5 req/sec).
- РўР°Р№РјР°СѓС‚ 10 СЃРµРє РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ.

РљСЌС€-СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ:
- РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ вЂ” TTL 7 РґРЅРµР№.
- 404 вЂ” С‚РѕР¶Рµ РєСЌС€РёСЂСѓРµРј, РЅРѕ TTL 1 РґРµРЅСЊ (negative caching, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
  РЅРµ РґС‘СЂРіР°С‚СЊ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕ, РЅРѕ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏС‚СЊ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рѕ).
"""
import json
import ssl
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

import certifi

from paradox.integrations.base import EnrichmentResult


_SSL_CONTEXT = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=certifi.where())


class ShodanInternetDBEnricher:
    name = "shodan_internetdb"
    supports = ("ip",)
    ttl_days = 7
    ttl_days_negative = 1

    base_url = "https://internetdb.shodan.io"
    timeout = 10.0
    user_agent = "Paradox/0.6.0"

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> bool:
        """Shodan InternetDB вЂ” Р±РµР· РєР»СЋС‡Р°, РІСЃРµРіРґР° РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ."""
        return True

    def lookup(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> EnrichmentResult:
        if ioc_type not in self.supports:
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                error=f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}",
                fetched_at=time.time(),
                ttl_days=self.ttl_days,
            )

        data, error = self._fetch(ioc)
        if error is not None:
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                error=error,
                fetched_at=time.time(),
                ttl_days=self.ttl_days,
            )

        # data is None в†’ 404, РЅРµС‚ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ
        if data is None:
            return EnrichmentResult(
                source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
                data={},
                fetched_at=time.time(),
                ttl_days=self.ttl_days_negative,
            )

        return EnrichmentResult(
            source=self.name, ioc=ioc, ioc_type=ioc_type,
            data=data,
            fetched_at=time.time(),
            ttl_days=self.ttl_days,
        )

    def _fetch(self, ip: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        """
        Р’РµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ (data, error).
        data=None + error=None вЂ” 404 (РЅРµС‚ РІ Р±Р°Р·Рµ).
        data=None + error="..." вЂ” СЃРµС‚РµРІР°СЏ РѕС€РёР±РєР° РёР»Рё 5xx.
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}/{ip}"
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={
            "User-Agent": self.user_agent,
            "Accept": "application/json",
        })
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(
                req, timeout=self.timeout, context=_SSL_CONTEXT,
            ) as resp:
                raw = resp.read()
                return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")), None
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            if e.code == 404:
                return None, None
            return None, f"HTTP {e.code}"
        except urllib.error.URLError as e:
            return None, f"URLError: {e.reason}"
        except TimeoutError:
            return None, "timeout"
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            return None, f"invalid json: {e}"
        except Exception as e:
            return None, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"


# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\threatfox.py
# ==========================================
"""ThreatFox (abuse.ch) вЂ” IOC РѕС‚ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµСЃС‚РІР°.

РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ Auth-Key. POST JSON: {"query": "search_ioc", "search_term": "..."}
"""
import os

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class ThreatFoxEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "threatfox"
    supports = ("ip", "domain", "hash")
    ttl_days = 1
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://threatfox-api.abuse.ch/api/v1/"
    timeout = 10.0

    def __init__(self, auth_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.auth_key = auth_key or os.environ.get("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY")

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.auth_key)

    def disabled_reason(self) -> str:
        return "ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY not set"

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        if ioc_type not in self.supports:
            return None, f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}"
        data, error = self._http_post_json(
            self.base_url,
            json_body={"query": "search_ioc", "search_term": ioc},
            headers={"Auth-Key": self.auth_key or ""},
        )
        if error is None and isinstance(data, dict):
            status = data.get("query_status")
            if status in ("no_result", "illegal_search_term"):
                return None, None
        return data, error



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\urlhaus.py
# ==========================================
"""URLhaus (abuse.ch) вЂ” malware URLs.

РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ Auth-Key (Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Auth-Key). Р‘РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ, СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ:
https://auth.abuse.ch/

POST form:
  host=example.com    в†’ /v1/host/
  url=https://...     в†’ /v1/url/
"""
import os

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class URLhausEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "urlhaus"
    supports = ("domain", "url")
    ttl_days = 1
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://urlhaus-api.abuse.ch/v1"
    timeout = 10.0

    def __init__(self, auth_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.auth_key = auth_key or os.environ.get("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY")

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.auth_key)

    def disabled_reason(self) -> str:
        return "ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY not set"

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        if ioc_type == "domain":
            url = f"{self.base_url}/host/"
            form = {"host": ioc}
        elif ioc_type == "url":
            url = f"{self.base_url}/url/"
            form = {"url": ioc}
        else:
            return None, f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}"

        data, error = self._http_post_json(
            url, form=form, headers={"Auth-Key": self.auth_key or ""},
        )
        # URLhaus: query_status == "no_results" вЂ” РЅРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С….
        if error is None and isinstance(data, dict):
            status = data.get("query_status")
            if status == "no_results":
                return None, None
        return data, error



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\urlscan.py
# ==========================================
"""URLScan.io вЂ” СЃРєР°РЅС‹ РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ Рё URL.

Search API Р±РµР· РєР»СЋС‡Р° (rate limit Р¶С‘СЃС‚РєРёР№). GET:
  https://urlscan.io/api/v1/search/?q=domain:example.com

Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёС… СЃРєР°РЅРѕРІ. РќРµ РїРѕ СЃР°РјРѕРјСѓ IOC вЂ” РїРѕ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃСѓ.
"""
import urllib.parse

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class URLScanEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "urlscan"
    supports = ("domain", "url")
    ttl_days = 1
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://urlscan.io/api/v1/search"
    timeout = 12.0

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        if ioc_type == "domain":
            query = f"domain:{ioc}"
        elif ioc_type == "url":
            query = f"page.url:{ioc}"
        else:
            return None, f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}"

        q = urllib.parse.quote(query)
        url = f"{self.base_url}/?q={q}&size=10"
        data, error = self._http_get_json(url)
        if error is None and isinstance(data, dict):
            if not data.get("results"):
                return None, None
        return data, error



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\integrations\virustotal.py
# ==========================================
"""VirusTotal API v3 вЂ” СЂРµРїСѓС‚Р°С†РёСЏ IP, РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ, С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ.

Free tier: 4 Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР°/РјРёРЅ, 500/РґРµРЅСЊ. РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ VIRUSTOTAL_API_KEY.

РџРѕРєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚:
  - IP: https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3/ip_addresses/{ip}
  - Р”РѕРјРµРЅ: https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3/domains/{domain}
  - SHA256: https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3/files/{hash}
"""
import os

from paradox.integrations.base_enricher import BaseEnricher


class VirusTotalEnricher(BaseEnricher):
    name = "virustotal"
    supports = ("ip", "domain", "hash")
    ttl_days = 1
    ttl_days_negative = 1
    base_url = "https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3"
    timeout = 15.0

    def __init__(self, api_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("VIRUSTOTAL_API_KEY")

    @property
    def enabled(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.api_key)

    def disabled_reason(self) -> str:
        return "VIRUSTOTAL_API_KEY not set"

    def _fetch(self, ioc: str, ioc_type: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
        endpoints = {
            "ip": "ip_addresses",
            "domain": "domains",
            "hash": "files",
        }
        section = endpoints.get(ioc_type)
        if section is None:
            return None, f"unsupported ioc_type: {ioc_type}"

        url = f"{self.base_url}/{section}/{ioc}"
        data, error = self._http_get_json(
            url, headers={"x-apikey": self.api_key or ""},
        )
        return self._resolve_not_found(data, error)

# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\reporters\flow_report.py
# ==========================================
from collections import Counter

from paradox.config import Config
from paradox.models import CaptureState, FlowKey, FlowStats, get_session_sni
from paradox.net_utils import classify_direction


def _flow_to_dict(
    key: FlowKey,
    flow: FlowStats,
    state: CaptureState,
    config: Config,
) -> dict:
    d = {
        "proto": key.proto,
        "src_ip": key.src_ip,
        "src_port": key.src_port,
        "dst_ip": key.dst_ip,
        "dst_port": key.dst_port,
        "direction": classify_direction(key.src_ip, key.dst_ip, config),
        "packets": flow.packets,
        "bytes": flow.bytes_sent,
        "duration": round(flow.duration, 6),
        "first_ts": flow.first_ts,
        "last_ts": flow.last_ts,
    }

    sni = get_session_sni(key, flow, state)
    if sni:
        d["sni"] = sni
    if flow.ja3:
        d["ja3"] = flow.ja3
    if flow.http_host:
        d["http_host"] = flow.http_host
    if flow.http_user_agent:
        d["http_user_agent"] = flow.http_user_agent
    return d


def _sort_key_bytes(f: dict) -> tuple:
    return (
        -f["bytes"],
        f["src_ip"],
        f["src_port"] or 0,
        f["dst_ip"],
        f["dst_port"] or 0,
        f["proto"],
    )


def _sort_key_packets(f: dict) -> tuple:
    return (
        -f["packets"],
        f["src_ip"],
        f["src_port"] or 0,
        f["dst_ip"],
        f["dst_port"] or 0,
        f["proto"],
    )


def build_flow_report(
    state: CaptureState,
    config: Config,
    top_n: int = 100,
) -> dict:
    all_flows = [
        _flow_to_dict(k, v, state, config)
        for k, v in state.flows.items()
    ]

    by_bytes = sorted(all_flows, key=_sort_key_bytes)[:top_n]
    by_packets = sorted(all_flows, key=_sort_key_packets)[:top_n]

    return {
        "total_flows": len(all_flows),
        "top_by_bytes": by_bytes,
        "top_by_packets": by_packets,
    }


def build_dns_report(
    state: CaptureState,
    top_n: int = 100,
) -> dict:
    """
    DNS-СЃРµРєС†РёСЏ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°.

    Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ + С‚РѕРї РїРѕ С‡Р°СЃС‚РѕС‚Рµ.
    РџР»СЋСЃ NXDOMAIN РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ.
    """
    all_queries = [
        {"name": name, "count": count}
        for name, count in state.dns_queries.items()
    ]

    by_count = sorted(
        all_queries,
        key=lambda d: (-d["count"], d["name"]),
    )[:top_n]

    nxdomain = [
        {"name": name, "count": count}
        for name, count in state.dns_nxdomain.items()
    ]
    nxdomain.sort(key=lambda d: (-d["count"], d["name"]))

    return {
        "unique_count": len(state.dns_queries),
        "total_queries": sum(state.dns_queries.values()),
        "top_by_count": by_count,
        "nxdomain": nxdomain,
    }


def build_http_report(
    state: CaptureState,
    top_n: int = 50,
) -> dict:
    """
    HTTP-СЃРµРєС†РёСЏ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°.

    РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ top hosts, top user-agents, top URIs Рё unique counts
    РёР· http_*-РїРѕР»РµР№ РІ FlowStats.
    """
    hosts: Counter = Counter()
    user_agents: Counter = Counter()
    methods: Counter = Counter()
    uris: list[dict] = []

    for key, flow in state.flows.items():
        if flow.http_host:
            hosts[flow.http_host] += 1
        if flow.http_user_agent:
            user_agents[flow.http_user_agent] += 1
        if flow.http_method:
            methods[flow.http_method] += 1
        if flow.http_uri and flow.http_host:
            uris.append({
                "method": flow.http_method,
                "host": flow.http_host,
                "uri": flow.http_uri,
                "src_ip": key.src_ip,
                "dst_ip": key.dst_ip,
                "dst_port": key.dst_port,
                "bytes": flow.bytes_sent,
                "user_agent": flow.http_user_agent,
            })

    # РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ URI: СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ bytes СѓР±С‹РІ., Р±РµСЂС‘Рј top_n
    # Р”РµРґСѓРїР»РёС†РёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ (host, uri) вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№
    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    unique_uris: list[dict] = []
    for u in sorted(uris, key=lambda x: -x["bytes"]):
        k = (u["host"], u["uri"])
        if k in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(k)
        unique_uris.append(u)
        if len(unique_uris) >= top_n:
            break

    return {
        "unique_hosts": len(hosts),
        "unique_user_agents": len(user_agents),
        "unique_uris": len(seen),
        "top_hosts": [
            {"host": h, "count": c}
            for h, c in sorted(hosts.items(),
                                 key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:top_n]
        ],
        "top_user_agents": [
            {"user_agent": ua, "count": c}
            for ua, c in sorted(user_agents.items(),
                                  key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:top_n]
        ],
        "methods": [
            {"method": m, "count": c}
            for m, c in sorted(methods.items(),
                                 key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))
        ],
        "top_uris": unique_uris,
    }


def build_http_object_report(
    state: CaptureState,
    top_n: int = 100,
) -> dict:
    """
    РћС‚С‡С‘С‚ РїРѕ HTTP-РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚Р°Рј, РёР·РІР»РµС‡С‘РЅРЅС‹Рј РёР· СЃРєР»РµРµРЅРЅС‹С… TCP-РїРѕС‚РѕРєРѕРІ
    (HTTPObjectExtractor). РљР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚ вЂ” (FlowKey, HttpObject).

    Top_n РїРѕ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂСѓ body. Body-РїСЂРµРІСЊСЋ вЂ” hex РїРµСЂРІС‹С… 64 Р±Р°Р№С‚.
    """
    objs = list(state.http_objects or [])

    # РЎРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° РїРѕ body_size (РєСЂСѓРїРЅС‹Рµ СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ), Р·Р°С‚РµРј РїРѕ uri/method
    objs.sort(
        key=lambda pair: (-pair[1].body_size, str(pair[1].uri or "")),
    )
    objs = objs[:top_n]

    out: list[dict] = []
    for key, obj in objs:
        headers = obj.headers or {}
        d = {
            "src_ip": key.src_ip,
            "dst_ip": key.dst_ip,
            "src_port": key.src_port,
            "dst_port": key.dst_port,
            "kind": obj.kind,
            "method": obj.method,
            "uri": obj.uri,
            "status_code": obj.status_code,
            "host": headers.get("host"),
            "user_agent": headers.get("user-agent"),
            "content_type": headers.get("content-type"),
            "body_size": obj.body_size,
            "truncated": obj.truncated,
        }
        if obj.body:
            # РџРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 64 Р±Р°Р№С‚Р° body вЂ” hex. Р”Р»СЏ Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅС‹С… РґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ,
            # РґР»СЏ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹С… РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РґРµРєРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ.
            d["body_preview"] = obj.body[:64].hex()
        out.append(d)

    return {
        "total": len(state.http_objects or []),
        "objects": out,
    }



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox\reporters\ioc_reporter.py
# ==========================================
"""
IOC reporter: СЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ РєРѕРјРїСЂРѕРјРёСЃСЃР° РґР»СЏ EDR/SIEM.

РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹, IP, sha256 Рё URL РёР· РіРѕС‚РѕРІРѕРіРѕ dict-РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°
(РІС‹С…РѕРґ run_pipeline) Рё РїРёС€РµС‚ РїСЏС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ:

  iocs.txt        вЂ” РїРѕСЃС‚СЂРѕС‡РЅРѕ, РґР»СЏ grep/copy-paste
  iocs.csv        вЂ” С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р°, РґР»СЏ Excel/LibreOffice
  iocs.json       вЂ” СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂР°, РґР»СЏ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёР·Р°С†РёРё
  suricata.rules  вЂ” РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р° IDS РґР»СЏ high-severity IOC
  queries.txt     вЂ” KQL (Sentinel/Defender) + Splunk

РќРёРєР°РєРёС… РІРЅРµС€РЅРёС… API. РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє вЂ” С‚Рѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ СѓР¶Рµ РІ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Рµ.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path


# РўРёРїС‹ findings, РёР· РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹С… РґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‘Рј РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹.
_DOMAIN_FINDING_TYPES = frozenset({
    "dga_domain",
    "cloud_tunnel",
    "cloud_tunnel_domain",
    "ip_check_service",
    "typosquatting",
    "sni_without_dns",
})

# РўРёРїС‹ findings, РёР· РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹С… РґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‘Рј IP (dst).
_IP_FINDING_TYPES = frozenset({
    "suspicious_port",
    "brute_force",
    "ftp_brute_force",
    "ssh_brute_force",
    "large_transfer",
    "smtp_spam",
    "ja3_malware",
    "beaconing",
    "lateral_movement",
    "reverse_asymmetry",
})

# РџСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ severity РґР»СЏ IOC.
_SEVERITY_RANK = {
    "critical": 4, "high": 3, "medium": 2, "low": 1, "info": 0,
}


def _sev_ge(sev: str, threshold: str) -> bool:
    """severity >= threshold."""
    return _SEVERITY_RANK.get(sev, 0) >= _SEVERITY_RANK.get(threshold, 0)


def _upsert_ioc(
    store: dict,
    key: str,
    new_entry: dict,
    sev_rank: dict,
) -> None:
    """
    Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ/РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ РІ store СЃ max-severity.

    Р•СЃР»Рё РєР»СЋС‡ СѓР¶Рµ РµСЃС‚СЊ вЂ” РїРѕРІС‹С€Р°РµРј severity РґРѕ РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕР№.
    Р­С‚Рѕ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРµРµ setdefault: РѕРґРёРЅ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ
    СЂР°Р·РЅС‹РјРё РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂР°РјРё СЃ СЂР°Р·РЅРѕР№ СѓРІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ.
    """
    existing = store.get(key)
    if existing is None:
        store[key] = new_entry
        return
    old_rank = sev_rank.get(existing.get("severity", "info"), 0)
    new_rank = sev_rank.get(new_entry.get("severity", "info"), 0)
    if new_rank > old_rank:
        existing["severity"] = new_entry["severity"]
        existing["source"] = new_entry["source"]


def _looks_like_ip(s: str) -> bool:
    """Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° 'РїРѕС…РѕР¶Рµ РЅР° IPv4'."""
    if not s or "." not in s:
        return False
    parts = s.split(".")
    if len(parts) != 4:
        return False
    try:
        return all(0 <= int(p) <= 255 for p in parts)
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _strip_port(value: str) -> str:
    """'203.0.113.66:4444' в†’ '203.0.113.66'."""
    if ":" in value and value.count(":") == 1:
        return value.rsplit(":", 1)[0]
    return value


def build_ioc_report(report: dict) -> dict:
    """
    РЎРѕР±СЂР°С‚СЊ IOC РёР· dict-РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°.

    Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚:
      {
        "domains": [{"value", "severity", "source"}],
        "ips":     [{"value", "severity", "source"}],
        "sha256":  [{"value", "source"}],
        "urls":    [{"value", "source"}],
      }
    Р”РµРґСѓРїР»РёС†РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ РїРѕ value, severity вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РёР· РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡РµРЅРЅС‹С….
    """
    domains: dict[str, dict] = {}
    ips: dict[str, dict] = {}
    sha256: dict[str, dict] = {}
    urls: dict[str, dict] = {}

    findings = report.get("findings") or []
    for f in findings:
        ftype = f.get("type", "")
        value = f.get("value") or ""
        sev = f.get("severity", "medium")

        if ftype in _DOMAIN_FINDING_TYPES and value:
            _upsert_ioc(
                domains, value,
                {"value": value, "severity": sev, "source": ftype},
                _SEVERITY_RANK,
            )

        elif ftype in _IP_FINDING_TYPES:
            # dst_ip вЂ” СЃР°РјС‹Р№ РІРµСЂРѕСЏС‚РЅС‹Р№ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂ.
            ip = f.get("dst_ip") or _strip_port(value)
            if ip and _looks_like_ip(ip):
                _upsert_ioc(
                    ips, ip,
                    {"value": ip, "severity": sev, "source": ftype},
                    _SEVERITY_RANK,
                )

        elif ftype == "vulnerable_path" and value:
            # value = URI, РІС‹С‚Р°С‰РёРј РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ URL РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ host.
            ev = f.get("evidence") or {}
            host = (ev.get("hosts") or [None])[0] if ev.get("hosts") else None
            if host and value.startswith("/"):
                url = f"http://{host}{value}"
                _upsert_ioc(
                    urls, url,
                    {"value": url, "severity": sev, "source": ftype},
                    _SEVERITY_RANK,
                )

    # sha256 РёР· carved_files.
    for cf in (report.get("carved_files") or {}).get("files", []) or []:
        sha = cf.get("sha256")
        if sha and len(sha) == 64:
            sha256.setdefault(sha, {
                "value": sha,
                "source": cf.get("uri") or "carved",
            })

    def _sort(items: dict) -> list[dict]:
        return sorted(
            items.values(),
            key=lambda x: (-_SEVERITY_RANK.get(x.get("severity", "info"), 0),
                           x["value"]),
        )

    return {
        "domains": _sort(domains),
        "ips":     _sort(ips),
        "sha256":  sorted(sha256.values(), key=lambda x: x["value"]),
        "urls":    _sort(urls),
    }


def render_iocs_txt(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """РџРѕСЃС‚СЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚."""
    lines: list[str] = []
    if ioc["domains"]:
        lines.append("# Domains")
        for d in ioc["domains"]:
            lines.append(d["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["ips"]:
        lines.append("# IPs")
        for i in ioc["ips"]:
            lines.append(i["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["urls"]:
        lines.append("# URLs")
        for u in ioc["urls"]:
            lines.append(u["value"])
        lines.append("")
    if ioc["sha256"]:
        lines.append("# SHA256")
        for s in ioc["sha256"]:
            lines.append(s["value"])
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_iocs_csv(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """CSV: type,value,severity,source."""
    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf)
    w.writerow(["type", "value", "severity", "source"])
    for d in ioc["domains"]:
        w.writerow(["domain", d["value"],
                    d.get("severity", ""), d.get("source", "")])
    for i in ioc["ips"]:
        w.writerow(["ip", i["value"],
                    i.get("severity", ""), i.get("source", "")])
    for u in ioc["urls"]:
        w.writerow(["url", u["value"],
                    u.get("severity", ""), u.get("source", "")])
    for s in ioc["sha256"]:
        w.writerow(["sha256", s["value"], "", s.get("source", "")])
    return buf.getvalue()


def render_iocs_json(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """РЎС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ JSON."""
    return json.dumps(ioc, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)


def render_suricata_rules(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """
    Suricata-РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р° РґР»СЏ РґРѕРјРµРЅ/IP СЃ severity >= high.

    SID СЃ Р±Р°Р·РѕР№ 9000000 вЂ” РЅРµ РїРµСЂРµСЃРµРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃ РїСѓР±Р»РёС‡РЅС‹РјРё РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°РјРё.
    """
    lines: list[str] = [
        "# Paradox generated rules",
        "# SID base 9000000 вЂ” local use",
        "",
    ]
    sid = 9000000

    for d in ioc["domains"]:
        if not _sev_ge(d.get("severity", "medium"), "high"):
            continue
        sid += 1
        val = d["value"]
        lines.append(
            f'alert dns $HOME_NET any -> any any '
            f'(msg:"Paradox: malicious domain {val}"; '
            f'dns_query; content:"{val}"; nocase; '
            f'sid:{sid}; rev:1;)'
        )

    for i in ioc["ips"]:
        if not _sev_ge(i.get("severity", "medium"), "high"):
            continue
        sid += 1
        val = i["value"]
        lines.append(
            f'alert ip $HOME_NET any -> {val} any '
            f'(msg:"Paradox: suspicious IP {val}"; '
            f'sid:{sid}; rev:1;)'
        )

    lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_queries(ioc: dict) -> str:
    """
    KQL (Sentinel/Defender) + Splunk-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹.

    РђРЅР°Р»РёС‚РёРє РєРѕРїРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІ EDR/SIEM, РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµС‚ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёСЏ.
    """
    parts: list[str] = []

    doms = [d["value"] for d in ioc["domains"]]
    ips = [i["value"] for i in ioc["ips"]]
    shas = [s["value"] for s in ioc["sha256"]]

    parts.append("# KQL вЂ” Microsoft Sentinel / Defender")
    parts.append("")
    if doms:
        doms_list = ", ".join(f'"{d}"' for d in doms)
        parts.append("# Domains")
        parts.append("DeviceNetworkEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where RemoteUrl in ({doms_list})")
        parts.append("")
    if ips:
        ips_list = ", ".join(f'"{i}"' for i in ips)
        parts.append("# IPs")
        parts.append("DeviceNetworkEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where RemoteIP in ({ips_list})")
        parts.append("")
    if shas:
        shas_list = ", ".join(f'"{s}"' for s in shas)
        parts.append("# SHA256 вЂ” РїРѕРёСЃРє Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР° С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ")
        parts.append("DeviceProcessEvents")
        parts.append(f"| where SHA256 in ({shas_list})")
        parts.append("")

    parts.append("# Splunk SPL")
    parts.append("")
    if doms:
        doms_or = " OR ".join(f'query="{d}"' for d in doms)
        parts.append("# Domains")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({doms_or})')
        parts.append("")
    if ips:
        ips_or = " OR ".join(f'dest="{i}"' for i in ips)
        parts.append("# IPs")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({ips_or})')
        parts.append("")
    if shas:
        shas_or = " OR ".join(f'sha256="{s}"' for s in shas)
        parts.append("# SHA256")
        parts.append(f'index=* ({shas_or})')
        parts.append("")

    return "\n".join(parts)


def write_ioc_files(ioc: dict, output_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """
    Р—Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ РїСЏС‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ РІ output_dir.

    Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ {С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚: РїСѓС‚СЊ}. Р•СЃР»Рё РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ вЂ” РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ dict.
    """
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    has_any = any(ioc[k] for k in ("domains", "ips", "sha256", "urls"))
    if not has_any:
        return {}

    written: dict[str, Path] = {}
    mapping = {
        "txt": ("iocs.txt", render_iocs_txt),
        "csv": ("iocs.csv", render_iocs_csv),
        "json": ("iocs.json", render_iocs_json),
        "suricata": ("suricata.rules", render_suricata_rules),
        "queries": ("queries.txt", render_queries),
    }
    for fmt, (name, renderer) in mapping.items():
        content = renderer(ioc)
        if not content.strip():
            continue
        p = output_dir / name
        p.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        written[fmt] = p
    return written

# FILE: \paradox\reporters\json_reporter.py
# ==========================================
import json
from pathlib import Path


def write_json(report: dict, path: Path) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)


def write_markdown(report: dict, path) -> None:
    """Р—Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ Markdown-РІРµСЂСЃРёСЋ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°."""
    from paradox.reporters.markdown_reporter import render_markdown
    from pathlib import Path

    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(render_markdown(report))

РќРµ РїР°СЂСЃРёРј JSON РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ вЂ” СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµРј СЃ dict РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ.

def _fmt_bytes(n: int) -> str:
    """Р§РµР»РѕРІРµРєРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РµРјС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ."""
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):
        if n < 1024:
            return f"{n:.1f} {unit}" if unit != "B" else f"{n} {unit}"
        n /= 1024
    return f"{n:.1f} PB"


def _fmt_duration(seconds: float) -> str:
    """Р§РµР»РѕРІРµРєРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РµРјР°СЏ РґР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ."""
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds:.1f} СЃРµРє"
    if seconds < 3600:
        return f"{seconds / 60:.1f} РјРёРЅ"
    if seconds < 86400:
        return f"{seconds / 3600:.1f} С‡"
    return f"{seconds / 86400:.1f} РґРЅ"


def _fmt_rel(seconds: float) -> str:
    """Р’СЂРµРјСЏ РІ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Рµ H:MM:SS РґР»СЏ timeline."""
    if seconds < 0:
        seconds = 0
    h = int(seconds // 3600)
    m = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)
    s = int(seconds % 60)
    if h > 0:
        return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
    return f"{m}:{s:02d}"


def _short(s: str, n: int = 40) -> str:
    """РћР±СЂРµР·Р°С‚СЊ РґР»РёРЅРЅСѓСЋ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ СЃ РјРЅРѕРіРѕС‚РѕС‡РёРµРј."""
    if not s:
        return ""
    return s if len(s) <= n else s[: n - 1] + "вЂ¦"


def _fmt_host(host: str | None) -> str:
    """
    Host РёР· HTTP-С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹. Р•СЃР»Рё СЌС‚Рѕ IP-Р»РёС‚РµСЂР°Р» вЂ” РїРѕРјРµС‡Р°РµРј РєР°Рє unknown host.
    РРЅР°С‡Рµ вЂ” РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕРµ РёРјСЏ.
    """
    if not host:
        return "?"
    try:
        ip_address(host)
    except ValueError:
        return host
    return f"{host} (unknown host)"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# MITRE
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _mitre_for_finding(f: dict) -> str:
    """
    Instance-level mitre_techniques РїСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚РЅРµРµ type-map.

    large_transfer Р·РЅР°РµС‚ РїСЂРѕ T1041/T1105 СЃР°Рј (РїРѕ direction),
    Р° _MITRE_MAP Р·РЅР°РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕР±С‰РёР№ T1041.
    """
    techniques = f.get("mitre_techniques") or ()
    if techniques:
        return ", ".join(techniques)
    return _MITRE_MAP.get(f.get("type", ""), "вЂ”")


# РЎРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ: СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРµ РёРјСЏ, РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІ timeline/findings.
def _mitre_for(finding_type: str) -> str:
    return _MITRE_MAP.get(finding_type, "вЂ”")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# IOC extraction
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _extract_iocs(findings: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """РџСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕРµ РёР·РІР»РµС‡РµРЅРёРµ IOC РёР· findings."""
    domains: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    ips: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    ports: dict[int, list[str]] = {}

    for f in findings:
        ftype = f.get("type", "")
        value = f.get("value", "")

        if ftype in ("dga_domain", "cloud_tunnel_domain",
                     "ip_check_service", "typosquatting",
                     "sni_without_dns"):
            domains.setdefault(value, []).append(ftype)

        elif ftype == "suspicious_port":
            if ":" in value:
                ip, port_s = value.rsplit(":", 1)
                ips.setdefault(ip, []).append(ftype)
                try:
                    ports.setdefault(int(port_s), []).append(ftype)
                except ValueError:
                    pass

        elif ftype == "nxdomain_burst":
            ips.setdefault(value, []).append(ftype)

    return {"domains": domains, "ips": ips, "ports": ports}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# TL;DR
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _pick_first_with_role(entities: list[dict], role: str) -> str | None:
    for e in entities:
        if role in e.get("roles", []):
            return e.get("ip")
    return None


def _unique_finding_types(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    seen: list[str] = []
    for f in findings:
        t = f.get("type")
        if t and t not in seen:
            seen.append(t)
    return seen


def _chain_for(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """
    РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С‚РёРїС‹ findings РІ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєРµ kill-chain.

    РџСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ вЂ” С†РµРїРѕС‡РєР°, Р° РЅРµ confidence: Р°РЅР°Р»РёС‚РёРє РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РІРёРґРµС‚СЊ
    В«СЃРєР°РЅ в†’ RCE в†’ payload в†’ beaconВ», РґР°Р¶Рµ РµСЃР»Рё beacon confidence РІС‹С€Рµ.
    """
    seen: set[str] = set()
    ordered: list[str] = []
    for f in findings:
        t = f.get("type")
        if t and t not in seen:
            seen.add(t)
            ordered.append(t)
    return sorted(
        ordered,
        key=lambda t: (_CHAIN_ORDER.get(t, _CHAIN_UNKNOWN), t),
    )


def _render_tldr(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    """РљРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёР№ TL;DR: С‡С‚Рѕ РїСЂРѕРёР·РѕС€Р»Рѕ, РєС‚Рѕ Р°С‚Р°РєСѓРµС‚, С‡С‚Рѕ СЃРєР°С‡Р°РЅРѕ."""
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    entities = report.get("entities", {})
    internal = entities.get("internal", [])
    carved = report.get("carved_files", {}).get("files", [])
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []

    lines: list[str] = ["## TL;DR", ""]

    if not findings and not contradictions:
        lines.append("_Findings РЅРµС‚. РўСЂР°С„РёРє РІС‹РіР»СЏРґРёС‚ С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Рј РЅР° СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµ СЌРІСЂРёСЃС‚РёРє._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

    attacker = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "attacker_candidate")
    victim = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "victim_candidate")
    pivot = _pick_first_with_role(internal, "pivot")

    # РћСЃРЅРѕРІРЅРѕРµ РїСЂРµРґР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ вЂ” С†РµРїРѕС‡РєР° РІ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєРµ kill-chain.
    types = _chain_for(findings)
    types_str = " в†’ ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in types) if types else "вЂ”"

    if pivot:
        lines.append(
            f"**PIVOT:** `{pivot}` вЂ” СЃРєРѕРјРїСЂРѕРјРµС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ Рё Р°С‚Р°РєСѓРµС‚ РґР°Р»СЊС€Рµ. "
            f"РўРёРїС‹: {types_str}."
        )
    elif attacker and victim:
        lines.append(
            f"**РРЅС†РёРґРµРЅС‚:** `{attacker}` Р°С‚Р°РєСѓРµС‚ `{victim}`. "
            f"РўРёРїС‹: {types_str}."
        )
    elif victim:
        lines.append(f"**Р–РµСЂС‚РІР°:** `{victim}`. РўРёРїС‹: {types_str}.")
    elif attacker:
        lines.append(f"**РђС‚Р°РєСѓСЋС‰РёР№:** `{attacker}`. РўРёРїС‹: {types_str}.")
    else:
        lines.append(f"**Findings:** {types_str}.")
    lines.append("")

    # РљР»СЋС‡РµРІРѕРµ вЂ” СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє
    if pivot:
        lines.append(f"- **Pivot:** `{pivot}` (РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№, РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РЅРѕ)")
    if attacker:
        lines.append(f"- Attacker: `{attacker}` (internal)")
    if victim:
        lines.append(f"- Victim: `{victim}` (internal)")

    # Payloads
    for f in carved[:3]:
        uri = f.get("uri", "?")
        size = f.get("size", 0)
        sha = (f.get("sha256") or "")[:16]
        ext = f.get("ext") or "?"
        lines.append(
            f"- Payload: `{uri}` ({ext}, {size} B, sha256 `{sha}вЂ¦`)"
        )
    if len(carved) > 3:
        lines.append(f"- _(РµС‰С‘ {len(carved) - 3} payload'РѕРІ вЂ” СЃРј. Carved Files)_")

    # High-confidence findings
    high = [f for f in findings if f.get("confidence", 0) >= 0.85]
    if high:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"**High-confidence ({len(high)}):**")
        for f in high[:5]:
            ftype = f.get("type", "?")
            value = _short(f.get("value", "?"), 70)
            lines.append(f"- `{ftype}` вЂ” `{value}` ({f.get('confidence', 0):.2f})")

    # Contradictions, РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ вЂ” РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєР°СЏ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР° РЅР° СЃРµРєС†РёСЋ.
    if contradictions:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(
            f"**РџСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕСЂРµС‡РёСЏ:** {len(contradictions)} вЂ” СЃРј. СЃРµРєС†РёСЋ РЅРёР¶Рµ."
        )

    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Timeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _escape_cell(s: str) -> str:
    """Р­РєСЂР°РЅРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ | РґР»СЏ markdown-С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹."""
    return (s or "").replace("|", "\\|")


def _render_timeline(report: dict, top_n: int = 50) -> list[str]:
    """
    РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ findings + contradictions РїРѕ С„Р°Р·Р°Рј kill-chain.

    v0.8.0: С‡РёС‚Р°РµРј report["timeline"] вЂ” РїР»РѕСЃРєРёР№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№,
    РѕС‚СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕ ts. Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ phase (kill-chain), РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё
    С„Р°Р·С‹ вЂ” С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р° СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№. Fallback РЅР° СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРµ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ, РµСЃР»Рё
    timeline РІ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Рµ РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚.
    """
    timeline = report.get("timeline")
    if not timeline:
        return _render_timeline_fallback(report, top_n=15)

    lines: list[str] = ["## Timeline", ""]
    lines.append(
        "_РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ findings Рё РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕСЂРµС‡РёР№ РїРѕ С„Р°Р·Р°Рј kill-chain. "
        "Р’СЂРµРјСЏ вЂ” РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ._"
    )
    lines.append("")

    # t0 вЂ” РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј ts СЃСЂРµРґРё РІСЃРµС… СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№.
    t0 = min(e.get("ts", 0.0) for e in timeline)

    # Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂРѕРІРєР° РїРѕ phase, РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє С„Р°Р· вЂ” РїРѕ chain_order.
    by_phase: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    phase_order: dict[str, int] = {}
    for e in timeline:
        phase = e.get("phase", "other")
        by_phase.setdefault(phase, []).append(e)
        phase_order[phase] = e.get("chain_order", 999)

    ordered_phases = sorted(
        by_phase.keys(),
        key=lambda p: (phase_order.get(p, 999), p),
    )

    shown = 0
    for phase in ordered_phases:
        events = by_phase[phase]
        lines.append(f"### {phase} ({len(events)})")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(
            "| Р’СЂРµРјСЏ (rel) | Kind | РўРёРї | Subject | РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ | Conf | MITRE |"
        )
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for e in events:
            if shown >= top_n:
                break
            rel = _fmt_rel(e.get("ts", 0.0) - t0)
            kind = e.get("kind", "?")
            etype = _escape_cell(e.get("type", "?"))
            subject = _escape_cell(_short(e.get("subject") or "?", 20))
            desc = _escape_cell(_short(e.get("description") or "", 70))
            conf = e.get("confidence", 0.0)
            mitre = e.get("mitre_id") or "вЂ”"
            lines.append(
                f"| {rel} | `{kind}` | `{etype}` | `{subject}` | "
                f"{desc} | {conf:.2f} | {mitre} |"
            )
            shown += 1
        lines.append("")
        if shown >= top_n:
            remaining = len(timeline) - shown
            if remaining > 0:
                lines.append(
                    f"_вЂ¦Рё РµС‰С‘ {remaining} СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№ вЂ” СЃРј. JSON-РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚._"
                )
                lines.append("")
            break

    return lines


def _render_timeline_fallback(report: dict, top_n: int = 15) -> list[str]:
    """
    Fallback РґР»СЏ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚РѕРІ Р±РµР· report["timeline"] (РґРѕ v0.8.0).

    РџР»РѕСЃРєР°СЏ С…СЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ findings РїРѕ first_seen вЂ” РєР°Рє Р±С‹Р»Рѕ СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
    """
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    lines: list[str] = ["## Timeline", ""]

    with_ts = [f for f in findings if f.get("first_seen") is not None]
    if not with_ts:
        lines.append("_РќРµС‚ timestamps Сѓ findings._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

    t0 = min(f["first_seen"] for f in with_ts)
    sorted_f = sorted(with_ts, key=lambda f: f["first_seen"])

    lines.append("| Р’СЂРµРјСЏ (rel) | РўРёРї | Р—РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ | Conf | MITRE |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for f in sorted_f[:top_n]:
        rel = _fmt_rel(f["first_seen"] - t0)
        ftype = f.get("type", "?")
        value = _short(f.get("value", "?"), 60)
        conf = f.get("confidence", 0.0)
        mitre = _mitre_for_finding(f)
        lines.append(
            f"| {rel} | `{ftype}` | {value} | {conf:.2f} | {mitre} |"
        )
    if len(sorted_f) > top_n:
        lines.append(f"| ... | _(РµС‰С‘ {len(sorted_f) - top_n})_ | | | |")
    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Carved files (РѕРїС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_carved_files(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    carved = report.get("carved_files", {}).get("files", [])
    if not carved:
        return []

    lines: list[str] = [f"## Carved Files ({len(carved)})", ""]
    lines.append("_Metadata-only. РўРµР»Рѕ РЅР° РґРёСЃРє РЅРµ РїРёС€РµС‚СЃСЏ._")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| URI | Ext | Size | SHA256 | First bytes (hex) |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for f in carved:
        uri = _short(f.get("uri", "?"), 50)
        ext = f.get("ext") or "?"
        size = f.get("size", 0)
        sha = (f.get("sha256") or "")[:16]
        fb = _short(f.get("first_bytes_hex", "") or "", 32)
        lines.append(
            f"| `{uri}` | `{ext}` | {size} B | `{sha}вЂ¦` | `{fb}` |"
        )
    lines.append("")
    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Contradictions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

_SEVERITY_ICON: dict[str, str] = {
    "critical": "рџ”ґ",
    "high": "рџџ ",
    "medium": "рџџЎ",
    "low": "рџџў",
    "info": "вљЄ",
}


def _render_contradictions(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []
    if not contradictions:
        return []

    lines: list[str] = [f"## РџСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕСЂРµС‡РёСЏ ({len(contradictions)})", ""]
    lines.append(
        "_РќРµСЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё РјРµР¶РґСѓ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР°РјРё. РќРµ Р°С‚Р°РєРё, Р° СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ "
        "В«РѕРґРЅРѕ РёР· РґРІСѓС… РЅРµРІРµСЂРЅРѕ, СЂР°Р·Р±РµСЂРёСЃСЊВ»._"
    )
    lines.append("")

    for c in contradictions:
        sev = c.get("severity", "info")
        icon = _SEVERITY_ICON.get(sev, "В·")
        rule = c.get("rule", "?")
        subject = c.get("subject", "?")
        conf = c.get("confidence", 0.0)

        lines.append(f"### {icon} `{rule}` вЂ” `{subject}` ({conf:.2f})")
        lines.append("")

        hint = c.get("resolution_hint")
        if hint:
            lines.append(f"> {hint}")
            lines.append("")

        lines.append("**Р¤Р°РєС‚С‹:**")
        lines.append("")
        for f in c.get("facts", []):
            src = f.get("source", "?")
            pred = f.get("predicate", "?")
            val = f.get("value") or "(unknown)"
            ts = f.get("ts", 0.0)
            fc = f.get("confidence", 0.0)
            # finding_type РёР· evidence вЂ” РѕС‚Р»РёС‡Р°РµС‚ nxdomain_burst РѕС‚ port_scan
            # РІ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Рµ, РєРѕРіРґР° value РїСѓСЃС‚.
            ftype = (f.get("evidence") or {}).get("finding_type") or ""
            ftype_str = f" [{ftype}]" if ftype else ""
            lines.append(
                f"- `{pred}` в†ђ `{val}`{ftype_str} "
                f"(source=`{src}`, ts={ts:.1f}, conf={fc:.2f})"
            )
        lines.append("")

        causes = c.get("likely_causes") or []
        if causes:
            lines.append("**Р’РµСЂРѕСЏС‚РЅС‹Рµ РїСЂРёС‡РёРЅС‹:**")
            lines.append("")
            for cause in causes:
                lines.append(f"- {cause}")
            lines.append("")

    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main render
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def render_markdown(report: dict) -> str:
    """РЎРѕР±СЂР°С‚СЊ Markdown-РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚ РёР· dict run_pipeline."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    provenance = report.get("provenance", {})
    summary = report.get("summary", {})
    findings = report.get("findings", [])
    flows = report.get("flows", {})
    entities = report.get("entities", {})
    http = report.get("http", {})
    top_flows = flows.get("top_by_bytes", [])
    contradictions = report.get("contradictions") or []

    # --- header ---
    pcap_path = provenance.get("pcap_path", "?")
    pcap_name = pcap_path.split("\\")[-1].split("/")[-1]
    lines.append(f"# PCAP Triage Report: `{pcap_name}`")
    lines.append("")

    lines.append(f"**РРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРјРµРЅС‚:** Paradox v{provenance.get('tool_version', '?')}  ")
    lines.append(f"**Р¤Р°Р№Р»:** `{pcap_path}`  ")
    lines.append(f"**SHA256:** `{provenance.get('pcap_sha256', '?')}`  ")
    lines.append("")

    # --- TL;DR ---
    lines.extend(_render_tldr(report))

    # --- Absence ---
    lines.extend(_render_absence(report))

    # --- Timeline ---
    if findings:
        lines.extend(_render_timeline(report))

    # --- Carved files ---
    carved_section = _render_carved_files(report)
    if carved_section:
        lines.extend(carved_section)

    # --- summary ---
    lines.append("## РЎРІРѕРґРєР°")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append(f"- **РџР°РєРµС‚С‹:** {summary.get('packets', 0):,}")
    lines.append(f"- **Flows:** {summary.get('flows', 0):,}")
    lines.append(f"- **РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… DNS-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ:** {summary.get('unique_dns_queries', 0)}")
    lines.append(f"- **NXDOMAIN:** {summary.get('nxdomain_count', 0)}")
    lines.append(f"- **Findings:** {summary.get('findings', 0)}")
    if contradictions:
        lines.append(f"- **РџСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕСЂРµС‡РёР№:** {len(contradictions)}")

    first_ts = summary.get("first_ts")
    last_ts = summary.get("last_ts")
    if first_ts and last_ts:
        duration = last_ts - first_ts
        lines.append(f"- **РџРµСЂРёРѕРґ:** {_fmt_duration(duration)}")

    uploaded = summary.get("uploaded_bytes")
    downloaded = summary.get("downloaded_bytes")
    if uploaded is not None and downloaded is not None:
        lines.append(f"- **Uploaded (internal в†’ external):** {_fmt_bytes(uploaded)}")
        lines.append(f"- **Downloaded (external в†’ internal):** {_fmt_bytes(downloaded)}")

    extractor_errors = summary.get("extractor_errors") or {}
    if extractor_errors:
        lines.append(f"- **РћС€РёР±РєРё СЌРєСЃС‚СЂР°РєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ:** `{extractor_errors}`")
    lines.append("")

    # --- entities ---
    if entities:
        lines.extend(_render_entities(entities, findings))
        lines.append("")

    # --- NDP ---
    lines.extend(_render_ndp(report))

    # --- findings ---
    lines.extend(_render_findings(findings))

    # --- contradictions ---
    contradictions_section = _render_contradictions(report)
    if contradictions_section:
        lines.extend(contradictions_section)
        lines.append("")

    # --- HTTP ---
    if http:
        lines.extend(_render_http(http))
        lines.append("")

    # --- IOC ---
    iocs = _extract_iocs(findings)
    if iocs["domains"] or iocs["ips"] or iocs["ports"]:
        lines.append("## IOC")
        lines.append("")

        if iocs["domains"]:
            lines.append("**Р”РѕРјРµРЅС‹:**")
            lines.append("")
            for domain, reasons in sorted(iocs["domains"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{domain}` вЂ” {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

        if iocs["ips"]:
            lines.append("**IP:**")
            lines.append("")
            for ip, reasons in sorted(iocs["ips"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{ip}` вЂ” {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

        if iocs["ports"]:
            lines.append("**РџРѕСЂС‚С‹:**")
            lines.append("")
            for port, reasons in sorted(iocs["ports"].items()):
                lines.append(f"- `{port}` вЂ” {', '.join(reasons)}")
            lines.append("")

    # --- top flows ---
    if top_flows:
        lines.append("## РўРѕРї-10 flows РїРѕ Р±Р°Р№С‚Р°Рј")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Src | Dst | Dir | Proto | Bytes | SNI |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for fl in top_flows[:10]:
            src = f"{fl.get('src_ip', '?')}:{fl.get('src_port') or '-'}"
            dst = f"{fl.get('dst_ip', '?')}:{fl.get('dst_port') or '-'}"
            direction = fl.get("direction") or "вЂ”"
            proto = fl.get("proto", "?")
            b = _fmt_bytes(fl.get("bytes", 0))
            sni = _short(fl.get("sni") or "вЂ”", 60)
            lines.append(
                f"| `{src}` | `{dst}` | {direction} | {proto} | {b} | `{sni}` |"
            )
        lines.append("")

    # --- footer ---
    lines.append("---")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("_РћС‚С‡С‘С‚ СЃРіРµРЅРµСЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏР№ findings РіР»Р°Р·Р°РјРё: "
                 "СЌРІСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєРё РґР°СЋС‚ РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ, РЅРµ РїСЂРёРіРѕРІРѕСЂС‹._")
    lines.append("")

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Findings
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_findings(findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    lines: list[str] = []
    if not findings:
        lines.append("## Findings")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("_РќРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ._")
        lines.append("")
        return lines

def _finding_types_for(entity: dict, findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """РџСЂРµРѕР±СЂР°Р·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ findings_indices в†’ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє С‚РёРїРѕРІ."""
    idxs = entity.get("findings_indices", []) or []
    types: list[str] = []
    for i in idxs:
        if 0 <= i < len(findings):
            t = findings[i].get("type")
            if t and t not in types:
                types.append(t)
    return types


def _render_entities(entities: dict, findings: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """РЎРµРєС†РёСЏ 'РЎСѓС‰РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё' РІ Markdown."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    internal = entities.get("internal", [])
    external = entities.get("external", [])

    if not internal and not external:
        return []

    lines.append("## РЎСѓС‰РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё")
    lines.append("")

    if internal:
        lines.append(f"### Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ С…РѕСЃС‚С‹ ({len(internal)})")
        lines.append("")
        for e in internal:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            roles = e.get("roles", [])
            roles_str = ", ".join(f"`{r}`" for r in roles) if roles else "вЂ”"
            lines.append(f"**`{ip}`** вЂ” {roles_str}  ")

            fo = e.get("flows_out", 0)
            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            bo = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_out", 0))
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            lines.append(f"  {fo} flows out В· {fi} in В· в†“ {bi} В· в†‘ {bo}  ")

            mac = e.get("mac")
            hostname = e.get("hostname")
            username = e.get("username")
            realm = e.get("realm")
            if mac or hostname or username or realm:
                parts = []
                if hostname:
                    parts.append(f"hostname: `{hostname}`")
                if mac:
                    parts.append(f"MAC: `{mac}`")
                if username:
                    parts.append(f"username: `{username}`")
                if realm:
                    parts.append(f"realm: `{realm}`")
                lines.append("  " + " В· ".join(parts) + "  ")

            sni = e.get("sni_domains", [])
            if sni:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in sni[:5])
                more = f" _(+{len(sni) - 5})_" if len(sni) > 5 else ""
                lines.append(f"  SNI: {shown}{more}  ")

            dns = e.get("dns_names", [])
            if dns:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in dns[:10])
                more = f" _(+{len(dns) - 10})_" if len(dns) > 10 else ""
                lines.append(f"  DNS: {shown}{more}  ")

            fnd_types = _finding_types_for(e, findings)
            if fnd_types:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in fnd_types)
                lines.append(f"  Findings: {shown}  ")

            lines.append("")

    external_with_findings = [e for e in external if e.get("findings_indices")]
    if external_with_findings:
        lines.append(f"### Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёРµ IP СЃ findings ({len(external_with_findings)})")
        lines.append("")
        for e in external_with_findings:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            roles = e.get("roles", [])
            roles_str = ", ".join(f"`{r}`" for r in roles) if roles else "вЂ”"
            lines.append(f"**`{ip}`** вЂ” {roles_str}  ")

            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            fo = e.get("flows_out", 0)
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            bo = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_out", 0))
            lines.append(f"  {fi} flows in В· {fo} out В· в†“ {bi} В· в†‘ {bo}  ")

            mac = e.get("mac")
            hostname = e.get("hostname")
            if mac or hostname:
                parts = []
                if hostname:
                    parts.append(f"hostname: `{hostname}`")
                if mac:
                    parts.append(f"MAC: `{mac}`")
                lines.append("  " + " В· ".join(parts) + "  ")

            sni = e.get("sni_domains", [])
            if sni:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{d}`" for d in sni[:5])
                lines.append(f"  SNI: {shown}  ")

            fnd_types = _finding_types_for(e, findings)
            if fnd_types:
                shown = ", ".join(f"`{t}`" for t in fnd_types)
                lines.append(f"  Findings: {shown}  ")

            lines.append("")

    external_clean = [e for e in external if not e.get("findings_indices")]
    if external_clean:
        lines.append("### Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёРµ IP Р±РµР· findings вЂ” С‚РѕРї-5")
        lines.append("")
        for e in external_clean[:5]:
            ip = e.get("ip", "?")
            fi = e.get("flows_in", 0)
            bi = _fmt_bytes(e.get("bytes_in", 0))
            lines.append(f"- `{ip}` вЂ” {fi} flows in, {bi}")
        lines.append("")

    return lines


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# HTTP
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_http(http: dict) -> list[str]:
    """РЎРµРєС†РёСЏ 'HTTP' РІ Markdown."""
    lines: list[str] = []

    unique_hosts = http.get("unique_hosts", 0)
    unique_uris = http.get("unique_uris", 0)
    unique_ua = http.get("unique_user_agents", 0)

    if not unique_hosts and not unique_uris:
        return []

    lines.append("## HTTP")
    lines.append("")

    methods = http.get("methods", []) or []
    if methods:
        method_str = ", ".join(
            f"{m.get('method', '?')}Г—{m.get('count', 0)}" for m in methods
        )
        lines.append(f"**Methods:** {method_str}  ")
    lines.append(f"**Hosts:** {unique_hosts} В· **URIs:** {unique_uris} В· "
                 f"**User-Agents:** {unique_ua}  ")
    lines.append("")

    top_hosts = http.get("top_hosts", []) or []
    if top_hosts:
        lines.append("### РўРѕРї Hosts")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Count | Host |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for h in top_hosts[:10]:
            host = _fmt_host(h.get("host"))
            lines.append(f"| {h.get('count', 0)} | `{host}` |")
        lines.append("")

    top_uris = http.get("top_uris", []) or []
    if top_uris:
        lines.append("### РўРѕРї URIs")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("| Method | Host | URI | Src в†’ Dst | UA | Bytes |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
        for u in top_uris[:15]:
            method = u.get("method") or "?"
            host = _fmt_host(_short(u.get("host") or "?", 30))
            uri = _short(u.get("uri") or "?", 60)
            src = f"{u.get('src_ip', '?')}:?"
            dst = f"{u.get('dst_ip', '?')}:{u.get('dst_port') or '-'}"
            ua = _short(u.get("user_agent") or "", 40)
            b = _fmt_bytes(u.get("bytes", 0))
            lines.append(
                f"| `{method}` | `{host}` | `{uri}` | `{src}` в†’ `{dst}` | {ua} | {b} |"
            )
        lines.append("")

    top_ua = http.get("top_user_agents", []) or []
    if top_ua:
        lines.append("### РўРѕРї User-Agents")
        lines.append("")
        for ua in top_ua[:10]:
            count = ua.get("count", 0)
            agent = _short(ua.get("user_agent") or "?", 80)
            lines.append(f"- Г—{count} вЂ” `{agent}`")
        lines.append("")

    return lines

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# РќРѕРІС‹Рµ СЃРµРєС†РёРё (NDP Рё Absence)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_absence(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    absence = report.get("absence", {})
    dns = absence.get("dns_unanswered", [])
    tcp = absence.get("tcp_unanswered", [])

    if not dns and not tcp:
        return []

    lines = ["## РђРЅРѕРјР°Р»РёРё / РћР±РѕСЂРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё", ""]

    if dns:
        lines.append("### DNS Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р°")
        lines.append("| Р”РѕРјРµРЅ (QNAME) | Р—Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for d in dns:
            lines.append(f"| `{d.get('qname', '?')}` | {d.get('count', 0)} |")
        lines.append("")

    if tcp:
        lines.append("### TCP Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° (SYN)")
        lines.append("| Src IP | Dst IP | РџРѕСЂС‚ | РџР°РєРµС‚С‹ |")
        lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
        for t in tcp:
            lines.append(f"| `{t.get('src_ip', '?')}` | `{t.get('dst_ip', '?')}` | {t.get('dst_port', '?')} | {t.get('packets', 0)} |")
        lines.append("")

    return lines

def _render_ndp(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    ndp = report.get("ndp", {})
    total = ndp.get("total", 0)
    neighbors = ndp.get("neighbors", [])
    routers = ndp.get("routers", [])

    if total == 0:
        return []

    lines = [f"## IPv6 / NDP ({total} РїР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ)", ""]

    if routers:
        lines.append("### Р РѕСѓС‚РµСЂС‹ (RA)")
        for r in routers:
            lines.append(f"- `{r}`")
        lines.append("")

    if neighbors:
        lines.append("### РЎРѕСЃРµРґРё (NDP)")
        lines.append("| IPv6 | MAC |")
        lines.append("|---|---|")
        for n in neighbors:
            lines.append(f"| `{n.get('ip', '?')}` | `{n.get('mac', '?')}` |")
        lines.append("")

    return lines
